\begin{table*}[t]
\centering
\caption{Spearman Rank Correlation Matrix ($\rho$) across Key Systems Telemetry Dimensions on Apple Silicon M4. Contrasts Regime A (Cold Prefill, $n=600$) and Regime B (Warm KV-Cache Reuse, $n=600$). In Regime A, prompt attention computation dominates global latency ($T_{\text{task}}$) and energy ($E_{\text{net}}$), yielding near-perfect rank alignment with token length ($\rho > 0.95$). In Regime B, KV-cache amortization unmasks runtime execution dynamics, where retired CPU instructions and dispatch latency strongly decouple from prompt prefill. Statistical significance: $^{***}p < 0.001$, $^{**}p < 0.01$, $^{*}p < 0.05$.}
\label{tab:spearman_correlation}
\small
\begin{tabular}{l r r r r}
\hline
\textbf{Systems Interaction} & \textbf{Regime A ($\rho_{\text{cold}}$)} & \textbf{$p$-value (A)} & \textbf{Regime B ($\rho_{\text{warm}}$)} & \textbf{$p$-value (B)} \\
\hline
$T_{\text{task}}$ vs $T_{\text{neural}}$ (Inference Latency) & +0.991$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ & +0.983$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ \\
$T_{\text{task}}$ vs $N_{\text{prompt}}$ (Prompt Token Length) & +0.948$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ & +0.586$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ \\
$T_{\text{task}}$ vs $E_{\text{net}}$ (Net Package Energy) & +0.930$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ & +0.960$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ \\
$T_{\text{task}}$ vs EDP (Energy-Delay Product) & +0.966$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ & +0.974$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ \\
$T_{\text{task}}$ vs $T_{\text{orch}}$ (Orchestrator Overhead) & +0.597$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ & +0.544$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ \\
$T_{\text{neural}}$ vs $N_{\text{prompt}}$ (Attention Prefill) & +0.955$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ & +0.584$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ \\
$E_{\text{net}}$ vs $N_{\text{prompt}}$ (Prefill Energy Drain) & +0.885$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ & +0.576$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ \\

Peak RSS vs Peak Physical RAM & +0.920$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ & +0.929$^{***}$ & $< 10^{-5}$ \\

FILE: .github/workflows/ci.yml
SHA-256: d8a7b2277ffbfc29a27b49142a156b1ece6e159bfd574a94f0a39799258e4ce1
================================================================================
name: Benchmark Suite CI / CD

on:
  push:
    branches: [ main ]
  pull_request:
    branches: [ main ]

jobs:
  test:
    name: Test & Verify (${{ matrix.os }})
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        os: [ubuntu-latest, macos-latest]

    steps:
      - name: Checkout Code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.12"
          cache: "pip"

      - name: Install Dependencies
        run: |
          python -m pip install --upgrade pip
          pip install ruff pytest numpy scipy matplotlib python-docx dulwich

      - name: Run Ruff Linter
        run: |
          ruff check benchmark/ tests/

      - name: Run Pytest Suite
        run: |
          pytest -v tests/

      - name: Run Reviewer Fast Dry-Run Integration Test
        run: |
          python3 benchmark/run_unified_benchmark.py --dry-run --output-dir /tmp/ci_dry_run

      - name: Run Cryptographic Manifest Verification
        run: |
          python3 scripts/verify_manifest.py

FILE: Makefile
SHA-256: 6ab2186272578a5912f96e00eff35d8baa31c988ec4fbe119a84edc1f79839d2

# ==============================================================================
# PicoClaw vs. OpenClaw Benchmark Suite — Unified Automation Makefile
# Suíte de Benchmark PicoClaw vs. OpenClaw — Makefile de Automação Unificada
#
# Author / Autor: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
# Platform / Plataforma: Apple Silicon M4 (macOS darwin-arm64)
# ==============================================================================

SHELL := /bin/bash
VENV_PY := $(shell if [ -f tools/.venv/bin/python ]; then echo tools/.venv/bin/python; else echo python3; fi)

.PHONY: help setup check-quiescence test verify dry-run simulate-campaign benchmark benchmark-full-smc reproduce-figures clean

help:
	@echo "========================================================================"
	@echo " 🚀 PicoClaw vs. OpenClaw: Comandos do Makefile / Makefile Targets"
	@echo "========================================================================"
	@echo " [PT-BR] Comandos disponíveis:"
	@echo "   make setup              - Configura venv Python, Node.js e ferramentas embarcadas"
	@echo "   make check-quiescence   - Audita estado térmico, CPU idle e modo anti-sono caffeinate"
	@echo "   make test               - Executa linter (ruff) e testes unitários automatizados"
	@echo "   make verify             - Valida integridade criptográfica dos artefatos (SHA-256)"
	@echo "   make dry-run            - Verificação rápida portátil para revisores (Modo Dry-Run / CI)"
	@echo "   make benchmark          - Executa campanha experimental completa (espaço de usuário comum)"
	@echo "   make benchmark-full-smc - Executa com sudo powermetrics para telemetria elétrica completa do SMC"
	@echo "   make reproduce-figures  - Regenera todas as figuras científicas em 300 DPI"
	@echo "   make clean              - Remove caches e arquivos temporários de execução"
	@echo ""
	@echo " [EN-US] Available targets:"
	@echo "   make setup              - Provisions isolated Python venv, Node.js, and embedded tools"
	@echo "   make check-quiescence   - Audits thermal state, CPU idle, and caffeinate anti-sleep"
	@echo "   make test               - Runs linter (ruff) and automated unit test suite"
	@echo "   make verify             - Validates cryptographic artifact integrity (SHA-256)"
	@echo "   make dry-run            - Reviewer fast portable dry-run verification (No Sudo / CI)"
	@echo "   make benchmark          - Runs publication-grade campaign (standard unprivileged user mode)"
	@echo "   make benchmark-full-smc - Runs with sudo powermetrics for full hardware electrical SMC telemetry"
	@echo "   make reproduce-figures  - Recompiles all 300-DPI scientific publication figures"
	@echo "   make clean              - Purges Python/Node caches and temporary execution files"
	@echo "========================================================================"

setup:
	@echo "📦 [PT-BR] Provisionando ambiente de teste..."
	@echo "   [EN-US] Provisioning testbed environment..."
	./scripts/setup_environment.sh

check-quiescence:
	@./scripts/check_quiescence.sh

test:
	@echo "🧪 [PT-BR] Executando linter (ruff) e testes unitários automatizados (pytest)..."
	@echo "   [EN-US] Running linter (ruff) and automated unit test suite (pytest)..."
	$(VENV_PY) -m ruff check benchmark/ tests/ scripts/
	$(VENV_PY) -m pytest -v tests/

verify:
	@$(VENV_PY) scripts/verify_manifest.py

dry-run:
	@echo "🧪 [PT-BR] Executando verificação rápida portátil para revisores (Modo Dry-Run)..."
	@echo "   [EN-US] Running fast reviewer portable dry-run verification..."
	$(VENV_PY) benchmark/run_unified_benchmark.py --dry-run

simulate-campaign: dry-run

benchmark:
	@echo "⚡ [PT-BR] Disparando campanha de benchmark em espaço de usuário comum..."
	@echo "   [EN-US] Dispatching benchmark campaign in unprivileged user space..."
	$(VENV_PY) benchmark/run_unified_benchmark.py

benchmark-full-smc: check-quiescence
	@echo "⚡ [PT-BR] Validando privilégios sudo para o leitor de sensores SMC..."
	@echo "   [EN-US] Validating sudo credentials for SMC powermetrics reader..."
	@sudo -n powermetrics -i 50 -n 1 >/dev/null 2>&1 || sudo -v
	@echo "⚡ [PT-BR] Disparando campanha com telemetria elétrica do Apple SMC em espaço de usuário comum..."
	@echo "   [EN-US] Dispatching full physical benchmark campaign in unprivileged user space..."
	$(VENV_PY) benchmark/run_unified_benchmark.py --reps 30

reproduce-figures:
	@echo "📊 [PT-BR] Regenerando figuras científicas em 300 DPI..."
	@echo "   [EN-US] Re-rendering 300-DPI scientific publication figures..."
	$(VENV_PY) benchmark/generate_charts.py

clean:
	@find . -type d -name "__pycache__" -exec rm -rf {} + 2>/dev/null || true
	@find . -type f -name "*.pyc" -delete 2>/dev/null || true
	@echo "🧹 [PT-BR] Caches temporários limpos."
	@echo "   [EN-US] Temporary caches purged."

SECAO 4: CODIGO-FONTE DOS ORQUESTRADORES E ADAPTADORES

================================================================================
FILE: benchmark/adapters/base.py
SHA-256: 65a4f485692ead175d82a48631f69051ed2caff44fa844a7c871db0ed4092098
================================================================================
"""
Base Abstract Adapter for AI Agent Orchestrators
Interface Base Abstrata para Adaptadores de Orquestradores de Agentes de IA

Author / Autor: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
Language / Idioma: Python 3.12+ (English source code, bilingual docstrings)
"""

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import Any, Dict, List


class AgentOrchestratorAdapter(ABC):
    """
    Abstract Base Class defining the contract for benchmarking AI agent orchestrators.
    Classe Base Abstrata que define o contrato para benchmark de orquestradores de agentes de IA.
    """

    def __init__(self, name: str, runtime: str, language: str, mode: str = "standard"):
        self.name = name
        self.runtime = runtime
        self.language = language
        self.mode = mode

    @abstractmethod
    def setup(self) -> bool:
        """
        Verify prerequisites, compile binaries or install dependencies.
        Verifica pré-requisitos, compila binários ou instala dependências.
        """
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def cold_start_cmd(self) -> List[str]:
        """
        Return the CLI command used for cold-start latency measurement via hyperfine.
        Retorna o comando CLI usado para medição de cold-start via hyperfine.
        """
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def run_workload(self, workload_id: str, prompt: str = "", iterations: int = 1) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Execute a standardized workload (W1-W4) and return structured telemetry.
        Executa uma carga de trabalho padronizada (W1-W4) e retorna telemetria estruturada.
        """
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def teardown(self) -> None:
        """
        Cleanup temporary session files, caches, or background processes.
        Limpa arquivos temporários de sessão, caches ou processos em segundo plano.
        """
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def metadata(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Return architectural and environment metadata for provenance tracking.
        Retorna metadados arquiteturais e de ambiente para rastreabilidade de proveniência.
        """
        pass



================================================================================
FILE: benchmark/adapters/picoclaw_adapter.py
SHA-256: b36463e8057a4d50e06d4dee45500db32578751acbc0c0395aa480f2298b2ebd
================================================================================
"""
PicoClaw Orchestrator Adapter (Go 1.23.2 Mach-O ARM64)
Adaptador do Orquestrador PicoClaw (Go 1.23.2 Mach-O ARM64)

Author / Autor: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
"""

import json
import os
import subprocess
from typing import Any, Dict, List

from benchmark.adapters.base import AgentOrchestratorAdapter
from benchmark.telemetry import run_subprocess_with_rusage


class PicoClawAdapter(AgentOrchestratorAdapter):
    """
    Adapter for Sipeed PicoClaw compiled native Mach-O ARM64 Go binary.
    Adaptador para o binário nativo Go Mach-O ARM64 compilado do Sipeed PicoClaw.
    """

    def __init__(self, repo_dir: str):
        super().__init__(
            name="PicoClaw",
            runtime="Go 1.23.2 (Native Mach-O ARM64)",
            language="Go",
            mode="native_static"
        )
        self.repo_dir = repo_dir
        self.bin_path = os.path.join(repo_dir, "benchmark", "picoclaw_orch")
        self.cli_path = os.path.join(repo_dir, "tools", "picoclaw", "picoclaw")

    def setup(self) -> bool:
        if not os.path.exists(self.bin_path):
            go_src = os.path.join(self.repo_dir, "benchmark", "picoclaw_orch.go")
            if os.path.exists(go_src):
                subprocess.run(
                    ["go", "build", "-o", self.bin_path, go_src],
                    cwd=os.path.join(self.repo_dir, "benchmark"),
                    check=True
                )
        return os.path.exists(self.bin_path)

    def cold_start_cmd(self) -> List[str]:
        if os.path.exists(self.cli_path):
            return [self.cli_path, "version"]
        return [self.bin_path, "version"]

    def run_workload(self, workload_id: str, prompt: str = "", iterations: int = 1) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        cmd = [self.bin_path, workload_id]
        stdout, stderr, rc, hw_stats = run_subprocess_with_rusage(cmd, cwd=self.repo_dir)
        if rc != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"PicoClaw execution failed (code {rc}): {stderr}")
        raw_output = stdout.strip()
        data = json.loads(raw_output)
        if isinstance(data, list) and len(data) > 0:
            res = data[0]
        else:
            res = data
        res["proc_instructions"] = hw_stats.get("proc_instructions", 0)
        res["proc_cycles"] = hw_stats.get("proc_cycles", 0)
        res["proc_billed_energy"] = hw_stats.get("proc_billed_energy", 0)
        res["proc_peak_phys_mb"] = hw_stats.get("proc_peak_phys_mb", 0.0)
        if res.get("rss_peak_mb", 0.0) == 0.0 and hw_stats.get("proc_rss_mb", 0.0) > 0.0:
            res["rss_peak_mb"] = hw_stats["proc_rss_mb"]
        return res

    def teardown(self) -> None:
        pass

    def metadata(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "runtime": self.runtime,
            "language": self.language,
            "mode": self.mode,
            "binary_path": self.bin_path,
            "gc_architecture": "Non-generational tri-color concurrent mark-sweep",
            "concurrency_model": "M:N lightweight Goroutines (~2 KB initial stack)"
        }

"""
OpenClaw Orchestrator Adapter (Node.js v26 / TypeScript on Google V8)
Adaptador do Orquestrador OpenClaw (Node.js v26 / TypeScript sobre Google V8)

Author / Autor: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
"""

import json
import os
from typing import Any, Dict, List

from benchmark.adapters.base import AgentOrchestratorAdapter
from benchmark.telemetry import run_subprocess_with_rusage


class OpenClawAdapter(AgentOrchestratorAdapter):
    """
    Adapter for OpenClaw orchestrator executing atop Node.js V8.
    Supports ablation modes: 'minimal' (equalized 3 tools) and 'full' (54 tools stock enterprise).
    """

    def __init__(self, repo_dir: str, mode: str = "minimal"):
        super().__init__(
            name="OpenClaw",
            runtime="Node.js v26.12.1 (Google V8 JIT)",
            language="TypeScript / JavaScript",
            mode=mode
        )
        self.repo_dir = repo_dir
        self.script_path = os.path.join(repo_dir, "benchmark", "openclaw_orch.mjs")

        # Locate node binary
        embedded_node = os.path.join(repo_dir, "tools", "node", "bin", "node")
        if os.path.exists(embedded_node):
            self.node_bin = embedded_node
        else:
            self.node_bin = "node"

    def setup(self) -> bool:
        return os.path.exists(self.script_path) and (os.path.exists(self.node_bin) or self.node_bin == "node")

    def cold_start_cmd(self) -> List[str]:
        return [self.node_bin, self.script_path, "--version"]

    def run_workload(self, workload_id: str, prompt: str = "", iterations: int = 1) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        env = os.environ.copy()
        env["OPENCLAW_MODE"] = self.mode
        cmd = [self.node_bin, self.script_path, workload_id]
        stdout, stderr, rc, hw_stats = run_subprocess_with_rusage(cmd, cwd=self.repo_dir, env=env)
        if rc != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"OpenClaw execution failed (code {rc}): {stderr}")
        raw_output = stdout.strip()
        data = json.loads(raw_output)
        if isinstance(data, list) and len(data) > 0:
            res = data[0]
        else:
            res = data
        res["proc_instructions"] = hw_stats.get("proc_instructions", 0)
        res["proc_cycles"] = hw_stats.get("proc_cycles", 0)
        res["proc_billed_energy"] = hw_stats.get("proc_billed_energy", 0)
        res["proc_peak_phys_mb"] = hw_stats.get("proc_peak_phys_mb", 0.0)
        if res.get("rss_peak_mb", 0.0) == 0.0 and hw_stats.get("proc_rss_mb", 0.0) > 0.0:
            res["rss_peak_mb"] = hw_stats["proc_rss_mb"]
        return res

    def teardown(self) -> None:
        pass

    def metadata(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "runtime": self.runtime,
            "language": self.language,
            "mode": self.mode,
            "tool_catalog_size": 54 if self.mode == "full" else 15,
            "node_binary": self.node_bin,
            "gc_architecture": "Generational mark-compact (Scavenger + Old Space)",
            "schema_validator": "TypeBox (@sinclair/typebox JSON-Schema)"
        }

FILE: benchmark/adapters/neural_baseline_adapter.py
SHA-256: 8fd0233383c636051e523aa655ae153711be99a107b2f8a4c0d16d9c6e7aef3f

"""
Neural Baseline Direct Adapter (llama.cpp Metal GPU Loopback)
Adaptador Direto de Linha de Base Neural (llama.cpp Metal GPU Loopback)

Author / Autor: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
Language / Idioma: Python 3.12+ (English source code, bilingual docstrings)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import time
import urllib.request
from typing import Any

from benchmark.adapters.base import AgentOrchestratorAdapter
from benchmark.python_orch import (
    build_tool_catalog,
    execute_tool_calc_stats,
    execute_tool_grep_logs,
    execute_tool_list_dir,
    execute_vector_rag,
)

REPO_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
BASE_DIR = os.environ.get("WORKLOAD_DATA_DIR", os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "data", "workload_data"))


class NeuralBaselineAdapter(AgentOrchestratorAdapter):
    """
    Direct neural inference adapter bypassing agent orchestration layers.
    Measures pure LLM inference latency directly via loopback HTTP (127.0.0.1:8080)
    executing the exact same sequence of HTTP completions, tool schemas (15 tools),
    and prompt payloads as the worker runtimes, ensuring identical prompt_tokens on Metal GPU.
    """

    def __init__(self, api_url: str | None = None):
        super().__init__(
            name="NeuralBaseline",
            runtime="llama.cpp b11887 (Metal GPU Shaders)",
            language="C++20 Native",
            mode="direct_neural",
        )
        self.api_url = api_url or os.environ.get("LLAMA_API_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:8080/v1/chat/completions")
        self.tools = build_tool_catalog(15)

    def setup(self) -> bool:
        health_url = self.api_url.replace("/v1/chat/completions", "/health")
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(health_url)
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=2) as resp:
                return resp.status == 200
        except Exception:
            return False

    def cold_start_cmd(self) -> list[str]:
        return ["curl", "-s", "http://127.0.0.1:8080/health"]

    def _call_stream(self, messages: list[dict], tools: list[dict], max_tokens: int) -> tuple[float, float, float, int, int, str, int, str]:
        payload = json.dumps({
            "model": "Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct-Q4_K_M.gguf",
            "messages": messages,
            "tools": tools,
            "max_tokens": max_tokens,
            "temperature": 0.0,
            "seed": 42,
            "stream": True,
            "cache_prompt": os.environ.get("BENCH_CACHE_PROMPT", "false").lower() == "true",
        }, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")

        t0 = time.monotonic()
        req = urllib.request.Request(self.api_url, data=payload, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
        prompt_ms = 0.0
        predicted_ms = 0.0
        has_server_timings = False
        ttft_ms = 0.0
        first_chunk = True
        token_count = 0
        cached_tokens = 0
        uncached_prompt_tokens = 0
        usage_prompt_tokens = 0
        completion_tokens = 0
        finish_reason = ""
        out_text = []

        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as resp:
            if resp.status != 200:
                raise RuntimeError(f"FATAL: llama-server returned HTTP {resp.status}")
            http_status = resp.status

            for raw_line in resp:
                line = raw_line.decode("utf-8").strip()
                if not line.startswith("data: "):
                    continue
                data_str = line[6:]
                if data_str == "[DONE]":
                    break
                try:
                    chunk = json.loads(data_str)
                    timings = chunk.get("timings")
                    if isinstance(timings, dict):
                        if "prompt_ms" in timings:
                            prompt_ms = float(timings["prompt_ms"])
                            has_server_timings = True
                        if "predicted_ms" in timings:
                            predicted_ms = float(timings["predicted_ms"])
                            has_server_timings = True
                        if "cache_n" in timings and int(timings["cache_n"]) >= 0:
                            cached_tokens = int(timings["cache_n"])
                        if "prompt_n" in timings and int(timings["prompt_n"]) >= 0:
                            uncached_prompt_tokens = int(timings["prompt_n"])
                        if "predicted_n" in timings and int(timings["predicted_n"]) > 0:
                            completion_tokens = int(timings["predicted_n"])

                    usage = chunk.get("usage")
                    if isinstance(usage, dict):
                        if "prompt_tokens" in usage and int(usage["prompt_tokens"]) > 0:
                            usage_prompt_tokens = int(usage["prompt_tokens"])
                        if "completion_tokens" in usage and int(usage["completion_tokens"]) > 0:
                            completion_tokens = int(usage["completion_tokens"])

                    choices = chunk.get("choices")
                    if choices and len(choices) > 0:
                        c0 = choices[0]
                        fr = c0.get("finish_reason")
                        if fr:
                            finish_reason = str(fr)
                        delta = c0.get("delta", {})
                        has_data = False
                        content = delta.get("content")
                        if content:
                            has_data = True
                            token_count += 1
                            out_text.append(content)
                        tool_calls = delta.get("tool_calls")
                        if tool_calls:
                            has_data = True
                            token_count += len(tool_calls)
                        if has_data and first_chunk:
                            ttft_ms = (time.monotonic() - t0) * 1000.0
                            first_chunk = False
                except Exception:
                    pass

        prompt_tokens = usage_prompt_tokens if usage_prompt_tokens > 0 else (cached_tokens + uncached_prompt_tokens)
        if has_server_timings:
            total_neural_ms = prompt_ms + predicted_ms
        else:
            total_neural_ms = (time.monotonic() - t0) * 1000.0

        if completion_tokens == 0:
            completion_tokens = token_count
        if not finish_reason:
            finish_reason = "stop"

        itl_ms = (total_neural_ms - ttft_ms) / float(completion_tokens - 1) if completion_tokens > 1 else 0.0
        return total_neural_ms, ttft_ms, itl_ms, prompt_tokens, completion_tokens, cached_tokens, uncached_prompt_tokens, finish_reason, http_status, "".join(out_text)

    def run_workload(self, workload_id: str, prompt: str = "", iterations: int = 1) -> dict[str, Any]:
        t_neural_total = 0.0
        ttft_first = 0.0
        itl_avg = 0.0
        total_prompt_tokens = 0
        total_completion_tokens = 0
        total_cached_tokens = 0
        total_uncached_prompt_tokens = 0
        last_finish_reason = ""
        last_http_status = 200

        mock_dir = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "mock_files")
        log_file = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "system_logs.jsonl")

        if workload_id == "W1":
            p = (
                "Analyze Apple Silicon M4 Unified Memory Architecture cache coherence and LPDDR5X bandwidth allocation. " * 8
                + "Summarize the primary latency determinant in two concise sentences."
            )
            msgs = [
                {"role": "system", "content": "You are a concise technical systems assistant."},
                {"role": "user", "content": p},
            ]
            tn, ttft, itl, ptok, ctok, c_tok, unc_tok, fr, hstatus, _ = self._call_stream(msgs, self.tools, 64)
            t_neural_total = tn
            ttft_first = ttft
            itl_avg = itl
            total_prompt_tokens = ptok
            total_completion_tokens = ctok
            total_cached_tokens = c_tok
            total_uncached_prompt_tokens = unc_tok
            last_finish_reason = fr
            last_http_status = hstatus

        elif workload_id == "W2":
            res1 = execute_tool_list_dir(mock_dir)
            vals, res2 = execute_tool_grep_logs(log_file)
            res3 = execute_tool_calc_stats(vals)
            p = f"System Tool Chain Results:\n1. list_dir: {res1}\n2. grep_logs: {res2}\n3. calc_stats: {res3}\nEvaluate the bus latency metrics and execute the next diagnostic step."
            msgs = [
                {"role": "system", "content": "You are a concise technical systems assistant."},
                {"role": "user", "content": p},
            ]
            tn, ttft, itl, ptok, ctok, c_tok, unc_tok, fr, hstatus, _ = self._call_stream(msgs, self.tools, 128)
            t_neural_total = tn
            ttft_first = ttft
            itl_avg = itl
            total_prompt_tokens = ptok
            total_completion_tokens = ctok
            total_cached_tokens = c_tok
            total_uncached_prompt_tokens = unc_tok
            last_finish_reason = fr
            last_http_status = hstatus

        elif workload_id == "W3":
            rag_context = execute_vector_rag(BASE_DIR)
            msgs = [
                {"role": "system", "content": "You are a concise technical systems assistant."},
                {"role": "user", "content": "Retrieved Context:\n" + rag_context + "\nQuestion: How do UMA bandwidth and runtime GC interact during local agent execution?"},
            ]
            tn, ttft, itl, ptok, ctok, c_tok, unc_tok, fr, hstatus, _ = self._call_stream(msgs, self.tools, 256)
            t_neural_total = tn
            ttft_first = ttft
            itl_avg = itl
            total_prompt_tokens = ptok
            total_completion_tokens = ctok
            total_cached_tokens = c_tok
            total_uncached_prompt_tokens = unc_tok
            last_finish_reason = fr
            last_http_status = hstatus

        elif workload_id == "W4":
            history = [
                {"role": "system", "content": "You are a concise technical systems assistant."},
            ]
            itl_sum = 0.0
            for step in range(1, 11):
                vals, _ = execute_tool_grep_logs(log_file)
                stat_str = execute_tool_calc_stats(vals)
                history.append({
                    "role": "user",
                    "content": f"Turn {step} Observation: {stat_str}. State next brief diagnostic action.",
                })
                tn, ttft, itl, ptok, ctok, c_tok, unc_tok, fr, hstatus, reply = self._call_stream(history, self.tools, 64)
                t_neural_total += tn
                if step == 1:
                    ttft_first = ttft
                itl_sum += itl
                total_prompt_tokens += ptok
                total_completion_tokens += ctok
                total_cached_tokens += c_tok
                total_uncached_prompt_tokens += unc_tok
                last_finish_reason = fr
                last_http_status = hstatus
                history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": reply})
            itl_avg = itl_sum / 10.0

        if total_prompt_tokens <= 0 or total_completion_tokens <= 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"FATAL: Validation failed - prompt_tokens ({total_prompt_tokens}) <= 0 or completion_tokens ({total_completion_tokens}) <= 0")

        # For Neural Baseline, task time is exactly neural inference time with 0 orchestrator overhead
        t_task_ms = t_neural_total
        cache_hit_pct = (total_cached_tokens / total_prompt_tokens * 100.0) if total_prompt_tokens > 0 else 0.0

        return {
            "architecture": "Neural Baseline (Direct llama.cpp)",
            "workload": workload_id,
            "warmth": "Cold",
            "t_task_ms": t_task_ms,
            "t_neural_ms": t_neural_total,
            "t_orch_ms": 0.0,
            "phi_orch_pct": 0.0,
            "ttft_ms": ttft_first,
            "itl_ms": itl_avg,
            "t_dispatch_ms": 0.0,
            "t_http_ms": 0.0,
            "r_tool_ops_sec": 0.0,
            "rss_idle_mb": 0.0,
            "rss_peak_mb": 0.0,
            "proc_instructions": 0,
            "proc_cycles": 0,
            "proc_billed_energy": 0,
            "proc_peak_phys_mb": 0.0,
            "alpha_mem": 1.0,
            "prompt_tokens": total_prompt_tokens,
            "completion_tokens": total_completion_tokens,
            "output_tokens": total_completion_tokens,
            "cached_tokens": total_cached_tokens,
            "uncached_prompt_tokens": total_uncached_prompt_tokens,
            "cache_hit_pct": cache_hit_pct,
            "tool_calls": 0,
            "http_status": last_http_status,
            "finish_reason": last_finish_reason,
        }

    def teardown(self) -> None:
        pass

    def metadata(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "runtime": self.runtime,
            "language": self.language,
            "mode": self.mode,
            "tool_catalog_size": 15,
            "endpoint": self.api_url,
            "gc_architecture": "None (C++ Manual Allocation)",
            "concurrency_model": "Native POSIX Threads (OpenMP / pthread)",
        }

"""
Bun Minimal Adapter for OpenClaw
Adaptador Bun Minimal para OpenClaw com suporte a fallback Node.js

Author / Autor: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
"""

import json
import os
import shutil
from typing import Any, Dict, List

from benchmark.adapters.base import AgentOrchestratorAdapter
from benchmark.telemetry import run_subprocess_with_rusage


class BunMinimalAdapter(AgentOrchestratorAdapter):
    """Adapter for OpenClaw Minimal executing atop Bun with Node.js fallback."""

    def __init__(self, repo_dir: str):
        super().__init__(
            name="BunMinimal",
            runtime="Bun / JSC (Fallback: Node.js V8)",
            language="TypeScript / JavaScript",
            mode="minimal",
        )
        self.repo_dir = repo_dir
        self.script_path = os.path.join(repo_dir, "benchmark", "openclaw_orch.mjs")
        local_node = os.path.join(repo_dir, "tools", "node", "bin", "node")
        self.runner_bin = shutil.which("bun") or (
            local_node if os.path.exists(local_node) else "node"
        )

    def setup(self) -> bool:
        return os.path.exists(self.script_path)

    def cold_start_cmd(self) -> List[str]:
        return [self.runner_bin, "--version"]

    def teardown(self) -> None:
        pass

    def metadata(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "runtime": self.runtime,
            "language": self.language,
            "mode": self.mode,
            "runner_binary_used": os.path.basename(self.runner_bin),
            "is_fallback_node": "bun" not in os.path.basename(self.runner_bin),
        }

    def run_workload(
        self, workload_id: str, prompt: str = "", iterations: int = 1
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        runner_base = os.path.basename(self.runner_bin)
        is_bun = "bun" in runner_base
        cmd = [self.runner_bin]
        if is_bun:
            cmd.append("run")
        cmd.extend([self.script_path, workload_id])

        stdout, stderr, rc, hw = run_subprocess_with_rusage(
            cmd,
            cwd=self.repo_dir,
            env={**os.environ, "OPENCLAW_MODE": "minimal"},
        )
        if rc != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"BunMinimal execution failed (code {rc}): {stderr}")

        res = json.loads(stdout.strip())
        res = res[0] if isinstance(res, list) and res else res
        res.update(
            {
                k: hw.get(k, 0)
                for k in (
                    "proc_instructions",
                    "proc_cycles",
                    "proc_billed_energy",
                    "proc_peak_phys_mb",
                )
            }
        )
        res["runner_binary_used"] = runner_base
        res["is_fallback_node"] = not is_bun
        return res

FILE: benchmark/adapters/__init__.py
SHA-256: 5aedd44265f63dd7d4f0504bb7bfccd0b05c843925029699137cca738f9ec2e7

"""
Adapters Package Initialization and Factory Function
Inicialização do Pacote de Adaptadores e Função Factory

Author / Autor: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
"""
from typing import Callable, Dict

from benchmark.adapters.base import AgentOrchestratorAdapter
from benchmark.adapters.bun_minimal_adapter import BunMinimalAdapter
from benchmark.adapters.neural_baseline_adapter import NeuralBaselineAdapter
from benchmark.adapters.openclaw_adapter import OpenClawAdapter
from benchmark.adapters.picoclaw_adapter import PicoClawAdapter
from benchmark.adapters.python_native_adapter import PythonNativeAdapter

ADAPTER_REGISTRY: Dict[str, Callable[[str], AgentOrchestratorAdapter]] = {
    "picoclaw": lambda repo_dir: PicoClawAdapter(repo_dir),
    "openclaw_minimal": lambda repo_dir: OpenClawAdapter(repo_dir, mode="minimal"),
    "openclaw_full": lambda repo_dir: OpenClawAdapter(repo_dir, mode="full"),
    "python_native": lambda repo_dir: PythonNativeAdapter(repo_dir),
    "neural_baseline": lambda repo_dir: NeuralBaselineAdapter(),
    "bun_minimal": lambda repo_dir: BunMinimalAdapter(repo_dir),
}


def get_adapter(name: str, repo_dir: str) -> AgentOrchestratorAdapter:
    """
    Instantiate and return an orchestrator adapter by registry key.
    Instancia e retorna um adaptador de orquestrador pela chave de registro.
    """
    key = name.lower()
    if key not in ADAPTER_REGISTRY:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown adapter '{name}'. Available: {list(ADAPTER_REGISTRY.keys())}")
    factory = ADAPTER_REGISTRY[key]
    return factory(repo_dir)


__all__ = [
    "AgentOrchestratorAdapter",
    "PicoClawAdapter",
    "OpenClawAdapter",
    "PythonNativeAdapter",
    "NeuralBaselineAdapter",
    "BunMinimalAdapter",
    "get_adapter",
    "ADAPTER_REGISTRY",
]

package main

import (
	"bufio"
	"bytes"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"math"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"regexp"
	"runtime"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"syscall"
	"time"
)

type FunctionParameterProp struct {
	Type        string `json:"type"`
	Description string `json:"description,omitempty"`
}

type FunctionParameters struct {
	Type       string                           `json:"type"`
	Properties map[string]FunctionParameterProp `json:"properties"`
	Required   []string                         `json:"required"`
}

type ToolFunction struct {
	Name        string             `json:"name"`
	Description string             `json:"description"`
	Parameters  FunctionParameters `json:"parameters"`
}

type ToolDefinition struct {
	Type     string       `json:"type"`
	Function ToolFunction `json:"function"`
}

type ChatMessage struct {
	Role    string `json:"role"`
	Content string `json:"content"`
}

type ChatRequest struct {
	Model       string           `json:"model"`
	Messages    []ChatMessage    `json:"messages"`
	Tools       []ToolDefinition `json:"tools,omitempty"`
	MaxTokens   int              `json:"max_tokens"`
	Temperature float64          `json:"temperature"`
	Seed        int              `json:"seed"`
	Stream      bool             `json:"stream"`
	CachePrompt bool             `json:"cache_prompt"`
}

type TrialResult struct {
	Architecture     string  `json:"architecture"`
	Workload         string  `json:"workload"`
	Warmth           string  `json:"warmth"`
	TTaskMs          float64 `json:"t_task_ms"`
	TNeuralMs        float64 `json:"t_neural_ms"`
	TOrchMs          float64 `json:"t_orch_ms"`
	PhiOrchPct       float64 `json:"phi_orch_pct"`
	TTFTMs           float64 `json:"ttft_ms"`
	ITLMs            float64 `json:"itl_ms"`
	TDispatchMs      float64 `json:"t_dispatch_ms"`
	THttpMs          float64 `json:"t_http_ms"`
	RToolOpsSec      float64 `json:"r_tool_ops_sec"`
	RSSIdleMB        float64 `json:"rss_idle_mb"`
	RSSPeakMB        float64 `json:"rss_peak_mb"`
	AlphaMem         float64 `json:"alpha_mem"`
	PromptTokens     int     `json:"prompt_tokens"`
	CompletionTokens int     `json:"completion_tokens"`
	OutputTokens     int     `json:"output_tokens"`
	CachedTokens     int     `json:"cached_tokens"`
	UncachedPromptTokens int `json:"uncached_prompt_tokens"`
	CacheHitPct      float64 `json:"cache_hit_pct"`
	ToolCalls        int     `json:"tool_calls"`
	HttpStatus       int     `json:"http_status"`
	FinishReason     string  `json:"finish_reason"`
}

func minInt(a, b int) int {
	if a < b {
		return a
	}
	return b
}

func getRSSMB() float64 {
	var rusage syscall.Rusage
	if err := syscall.Getrusage(syscall.RUSAGE_SELF, &rusage); err == nil {
		if runtime.GOOS == "darwin" {
			return float64(rusage.Maxrss) / (1024.0 * 1024.0)
		}
		return float64(rusage.Maxrss) / 1024.0
	}
	var m runtime.MemStats
	runtime.ReadMemStats(&m)
	return float64(m.Sys) / (1024.0 * 1024.0)
}

func buildStandardToolCatalog(count int) []ToolDefinition {
	tools := make([]ToolDefinition, count)
	for i := 0; i < count; i++ {
		tools[i] = ToolDefinition{
			Type: "function",
			Function: ToolFunction{
				Name:        fmt.Sprintf("system_tool_%d", i),
				Description: fmt.Sprintf("Standard system tool descriptor #%d for agent runtime evaluation.", i),
				Parameters: FunctionParameters{
					Type: "object",
					Properties: map[string]FunctionParameterProp{
						"action": {Type: "string", Description: "Action to execute"},
						"target": {Type: "string", Description: "Target parameter or file path"},
					},
					Required: []string{"action", "target"},
				},
			},
		}
	}
	return tools
}

func executeToolListDir(mockDir string) (string, error) {
	entries, err := os.ReadDir(mockDir)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	var names []string
	for _, e := range entries {
		info, _ := e.Info()
		sz := int64(0)
		if info != nil {
			sz = info.Size()
		}
		names = append(names, fmt.Sprintf("%s:%d", e.Name(), sz))
	}
	k := minInt(10, len(names))
	out, _ := json.Marshal(map[string]interface{}{
		"count": len(names),
		"files": names[:k],
	})
	return string(out), nil
}

func executeToolGrepLogs(logFile string) ([]float64, string, error) {
	f, err := os.Open(logFile)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, "", err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	re := regexp.MustCompile(`"subsystem":\s*"UMA_BUS_CONTROLLER"`)
	var vals []float64
	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(f)
	for scanner.Scan() {
		line := scanner.Text()
		if re.MatchString(line) {
			var obj map[string]interface{}
			if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &obj); err == nil {
				if v, ok := obj["latency_ms"].(float64); ok {
					vals = append(vals, v)
				}
			}
		}
	}
	k := minInt(5, len(vals))
	sample := []float64{}
	if k > 0 {
		sample = vals[:k]
	}
	out, _ := json.Marshal(map[string]interface{}{
		"matched_records": len(vals),
		"sample":          sample,
	})
	return vals, string(out), nil
}

func executeToolCalcStats(vals []float64) string {
	n := len(vals)
	if n == 0 {
		return "{}"
	}
	sum := 0.0
	for _, v := range vals {
		sum += v
	}
	mean := sum / float64(n)
	varSq := 0.0
	for _, v := range vals {
		d := v - mean
		varSq += d * d
	}
	variance := 0.0
	if n > 1 {
		variance = varSq / float64(n-1)
	}
	out, _ := json.Marshal(map[string]interface{}{
		"n":        n,
		"mean":     mean,
		"variance": variance,
		"stddev":   math.Sqrt(variance),
	})
	return string(out)
}

type docScore struct {
	idx   int
	score float32
}

type StreamResult struct {
	TotalNeuralMs        float64
	TTFTMs               float64
	ITLMs                float64
	PromptTokens         int
	CompletionTokens     int
	CachedTokens         int
	UncachedPromptTokens int
	FinishReason         string
	HttpStatus           int
	OutputText           string
}

func executeVectorRAG(baseDir string) string {
	const inDim = 256
	const outDim = 384
	const nDocs = 10000

	wProj := make([]float32, inDim*outDim)
	bProj := make([]float32, outDim)
	docMatrix := make([]float32, nDocs*outDim)

	wFile := filepath.Join(baseDir, "w_proj.bin")
	bFile := filepath.Join(baseDir, "b_proj.bin")
	docsBinFile := filepath.Join(baseDir, "doc_embeddings.bin")

	wBytes, errW := os.ReadFile(wFile)
	bBytes, errB := os.ReadFile(bFile)
	docBytes, errDoc := os.ReadFile(docsBinFile)
	if errW != nil || errB != nil || errDoc != nil ||
		len(wBytes) != inDim*outDim*4 || len(bBytes) != outDim*4 || len(docBytes) != nDocs*outDim*4 {
		panic(fmt.Sprintf("FATAL: Required RAG binary datasets missing or corrupted in %s (w_proj.bin, b_proj.bin, doc_embeddings.bin)", baseDir))
	}

	for i := 0; i < inDim*outDim; i++ {
		wProj[i] = math.Float32frombits(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(wBytes[i*4 : (i+1)*4]))
	}
	for i := 0; i < outDim; i++ {
		bProj[i] = math.Float32frombits(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(bBytes[i*4 : (i+1)*4]))
	}
	for i := 0; i < nDocs*outDim; i++ {
		docMatrix[i] = math.Float32frombits(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(docBytes[i*4 : (i+1)*4]))
	}

	// 1. Query vector initialization and linear projection (256 -> 384)
	qRaw := make([]float32, inDim)
	for d := 0; d < inDim; d++ {
		qRaw[d] = float32(math.Sin(float64(d)*0.17)) * 0.05
	}

	qProj := make([]float32, outDim)
	for j := 0; j < outDim; j++ {
		sum := bProj[j]
		for i := 0; i < inDim; i++ {
			sum += qRaw[i] * wProj[i*outDim+j]
		}
		qProj[j] = sum
	}

	// 2. L2 Normalization of projected query vector
	var normSq float32
	for j := 0; j < outDim; j++ {
		normSq += qProj[j] * qProj[j]
	}
	norm := float32(math.Sqrt(float64(normSq))) + 1e-8
	for j := 0; j < outDim; j++ {
		qProj[j] /= norm
	}

	// 3. Cosine similarity against all 10,000 document vectors
	scores := make([]docScore, nDocs)
	for i := 0; i < nDocs; i++ {
		var dot float32
		rowOffset := i * outDim
		for j := 0; j < outDim; j++ {
			dot += qProj[j] * docMatrix[rowOffset+j]
		}
		scores[i] = docScore{idx: i, score: dot}
	}

	// 4. Descending sort to select Top-5 chunks
	sort.Slice(scores, func(a, b int) bool {
		return scores[a].score > scores[b].score
	})

	docsFile := filepath.Join(baseDir, "rag_chunks_10k.json")
	raw, errChunks := os.ReadFile(docsFile)
	if errChunks != nil {
		panic(fmt.Sprintf("FATAL: rag_chunks_10k.json missing in %s: %v", baseDir, errChunks))
	}
	var chunks map[string]string
	_ = json.Unmarshal(raw, &chunks)

	var sb strings.Builder
	for k := 0; k < 5; k++ {
		topIdx := scores[k].idx
		if text, ok := chunks[fmt.Sprintf("%d", topIdx)]; ok {
			sb.WriteString(text)
		} else {
			sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("[Chunk #%04d] Context reference document.", topIdx))
		}
		sb.WriteString("\n")
	}
	return sb.String()
}

func callLlamaStream(client *http.Client, apiURL string, messages []ChatMessage, tools []ToolDefinition, maxTokens int) StreamResult {
	reqBody := ChatRequest{
		Model:       "Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct-Q4_K_M.gguf",
		Messages:    messages,
		Tools:       tools,
		MaxTokens:   maxTokens,
		Temperature: 0.0,
		Seed:        42,
		Stream:      true,
		CachePrompt: os.Getenv("BENCH_CACHE_PROMPT") == "true",
	}
	payload, _ := json.Marshal(reqBody)
	t0 := time.Now()
	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", apiURL, bytes.NewReader(payload))
	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	resp, err := client.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		panic(fmt.Sprintf("FATAL: HTTP request to llama-server failed: %v", err))
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()

	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		bodyBytes, _ := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
		panic(fmt.Sprintf("FATAL: llama-server returned HTTP %d: %s", resp.StatusCode, string(bodyBytes)))
	}

	var ttftMs float64
	var promptMs, predictedMs float64
	hasServerTimings := false
	firstChunk := true
	tokenCount := 0
	var cachedTokens, uncachedPromptTokens, usagePromptTokens, completionTokens int
	var finishReason string
	var outText strings.Builder
	reader := bufio.NewReader(resp.Body)

	for {
		line, err := reader.ReadString('\n')
		if err != nil {
			break
		}
		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "data: ") {
			dataStr := strings.TrimPrefix(line, "data: ")
			if dataStr == "[DONE]" {
				break
			}
			var chunk map[string]interface{}
			if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(dataStr), &chunk); err == nil {
				if timings, ok := chunk["timings"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {
					if pMs, ok := timings["prompt_ms"].(float64); ok {
						promptMs = pMs
						hasServerTimings = true
					}
					if predMs, ok := timings["predicted_ms"].(float64); ok {
						predictedMs = predMs
						hasServerTimings = true
					}
					if cn, ok := timings["cache_n"].(float64); ok && cn >= 0 {
						cachedTokens = int(cn)
					}
					if pn, ok := timings["prompt_n"].(float64); ok && pn >= 0 {
						uncachedPromptTokens = int(pn)
					}
					if predN, ok := timings["predicted_n"].(float64); ok && predN > 0 {
						completionTokens = int(predN)
					}
				}
				if usage, ok := chunk["usage"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {
					if pt, ok := usage["prompt_tokens"].(float64); ok && pt > 0 {
						usagePromptTokens = int(pt)
					}
					if ct, ok := usage["completion_tokens"].(float64); ok && ct > 0 {
						completionTokens = int(ct)
					}
				}
				if choices, ok := chunk["choices"].([]interface{}); ok && len(choices) > 0 {
					if c0, ok := choices[0].(map[string]interface{}); ok {
						if fr, ok := c0["finish_reason"].(string); ok && fr != "" {
							finishReason = fr
						}
						if delta, ok := c0["delta"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {
							hasData := false
							if content, ok := delta["content"].(string); ok && len(content) > 0 {
								hasData = true
								tokenCount++
								outText.WriteString(content)
							}
							if toolCalls, ok := delta["tool_calls"].([]interface{}); ok && len(toolCalls) > 0 {
								hasData = true
								tokenCount += len(toolCalls)
							}
							if hasData && firstChunk {
								ttftMs = float64(time.Since(t0).Nanoseconds()) / 1e6
								firstChunk = false
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	var totalNeuralMs float64
	if hasServerTimings {
		totalNeuralMs = promptMs + predictedMs
	} else {
		totalNeuralMs = float64(time.Since(t0).Nanoseconds()) / 1e6
	}

	promptTokens := 0
	if usagePromptTokens > 0 {
		promptTokens = usagePromptTokens
	} else {
		promptTokens = cachedTokens + uncachedPromptTokens
	}

	if completionTokens == 0 {
		completionTokens = tokenCount
	}
	if finishReason == "" {
		finishReason = "stop"
	}

	itlMs := 0.0
	if completionTokens > 1 {
		itlMs = (totalNeuralMs - ttftMs) / float64(completionTokens-1)
	}

	return StreamResult{
		TotalNeuralMs:        totalNeuralMs,
		TTFTMs:               ttftMs,
		ITLMs:                itlMs,
		PromptTokens:         promptTokens,
		CompletionTokens:     completionTokens,
		CachedTokens:         cachedTokens,
		UncachedPromptTokens: uncachedPromptTokens,
		FinishReason:         finishReason,
		HttpStatus:           resp.StatusCode,
		OutputText:           outText.String(),
	}
}

func runWorkload(client *http.Client, apiURL, baseDir, tier, warmth string, rssIdle float64) TrialResult {
	tStart := time.Now()
	var tNeuralTotal, ttftFirst, itlAvg, tDispatchTotal float64
	var totalPromptTokens, totalCompletionTokens, totalCachedTokens, totalUncachedPromptTokens, toolCalls int
	var lastFinishReason string
	lastHttpStatus := 200

	// Equalized tool definitions sent in request (15 tools for minimal/picoclaw parity)
	tToolCatalog0 := time.Now()
	toolCatalog := buildStandardToolCatalog(15)
	tCatalogMs := float64(time.Since(tToolCatalog0).Nanoseconds()) / 1e6

	mockDir := filepath.Join(baseDir, "mock_files")
	logFile := filepath.Join(baseDir, "system_logs.jsonl")

	switch tier {
	case "W1":
		tDispatchTotal = tCatalogMs
		prompt := strings.Repeat("Analyze Apple Silicon M4 Unified Memory Architecture cache coherence and LPDDR5X bandwidth allocation. ", 8) +
			"Summarize the primary latency determinant in two concise sentences."
		msgs := []ChatMessage{
			{Role: "system", Content: "You are a concise technical systems assistant."},
			{Role: "user", Content: prompt},
		}
		sr := callLlamaStream(client, apiURL, msgs, toolCatalog, 64)
		tNeuralTotal = sr.TotalNeuralMs
		ttftFirst = sr.TTFTMs
		itlAvg = sr.ITLMs
		totalPromptTokens = sr.PromptTokens
		totalCompletionTokens = sr.CompletionTokens
		totalCachedTokens = sr.CachedTokens
		totalUncachedPromptTokens = sr.UncachedPromptTokens
		lastFinishReason = sr.FinishReason
		lastHttpStatus = sr.HttpStatus
		toolCalls = 0

	case "W2":
		tTool0 := time.Now()
		res1, err1 := executeToolListDir(mockDir)
		if err1 != nil {
			panic(fmt.Sprintf("W2 executeToolListDir failed: %v", err1))
		}
		vals, res2, err2 := executeToolGrepLogs(logFile)
		if err2 != nil {
			panic(fmt.Sprintf("W2 executeToolGrepLogs failed: %v", err2))
		}
		res3 := executeToolCalcStats(vals)
		tDispatchTotal = tCatalogMs + float64(time.Since(tTool0).Nanoseconds())/1e6
		toolCalls = 3

		prompt := fmt.Sprintf("System Tool Chain Results:\n1. list_dir: %s\n2. grep_logs: %s\n3. calc_stats: %s\nEvaluate the bus latency metrics and execute the next diagnostic step.", res1, res2, res3)
		msgs := []ChatMessage{
			{Role: "system", Content: "You are a concise technical systems assistant."},
			{Role: "user", Content: prompt},
		}
		sr := callLlamaStream(client, apiURL, msgs, toolCatalog, 128)
		tNeuralTotal = sr.TotalNeuralMs
		ttftFirst = sr.TTFTMs
		itlAvg = sr.ITLMs
		totalPromptTokens = sr.PromptTokens
		totalCompletionTokens = sr.CompletionTokens
		totalCachedTokens = sr.CachedTokens
		totalUncachedPromptTokens = sr.UncachedPromptTokens
		lastFinishReason = sr.FinishReason
		lastHttpStatus = sr.HttpStatus

	case "W3":
		tTool0 := time.Now()
		ragContext := executeVectorRAG(baseDir)
		tDispatchTotal = tCatalogMs + float64(time.Since(tTool0).Nanoseconds())/1e6
		toolCalls = 1

		msgs := []ChatMessage{
			{Role: "system", Content: "You are a concise technical systems assistant."},
			{Role: "user", Content: "Retrieved Context:\n" + ragContext + "\nQuestion: How do UMA bandwidth and runtime GC interact during local agent execution?"},
		}
		sr := callLlamaStream(client, apiURL, msgs, toolCatalog, 256)
		tNeuralTotal = sr.TotalNeuralMs
		ttftFirst = sr.TTFTMs
		itlAvg = sr.ITLMs
		totalPromptTokens = sr.PromptTokens
		totalCompletionTokens = sr.CompletionTokens
		totalCachedTokens = sr.CachedTokens
		totalUncachedPromptTokens = sr.UncachedPromptTokens
		lastFinishReason = sr.FinishReason
		lastHttpStatus = sr.HttpStatus

	case "W4":
		toolCalls = 10
		var itlSum float64
		history := []ChatMessage{
			{Role: "system", Content: "You are a concise technical systems assistant."},
		}
		tDispatchTotal = tCatalogMs
		for step := 1; step <= 10; step++ {
			tTool0 := time.Now()
			_, _ = executeToolListDir(mockDir)
			vals, _, _ := executeToolGrepLogs(logFile)
			statStr := executeToolCalcStats(vals)
			tDispatchTotal += float64(time.Since(tTool0).Nanoseconds()) / 1e6

			history = append(history, ChatMessage{
				Role:    "user",
				Content: fmt.Sprintf("Turn %d Observation: %s. State next brief diagnostic action.", step, statStr),
			})
			sr := callLlamaStream(client, apiURL, history, toolCatalog, 64)
			tNeuralTotal += sr.TotalNeuralMs
			if step == 1 {
				ttftFirst = sr.TTFTMs
			}
			itlSum += sr.ITLMs
			totalPromptTokens += sr.PromptTokens
			totalCompletionTokens += sr.CompletionTokens
			totalCachedTokens += sr.CachedTokens
			totalUncachedPromptTokens += sr.UncachedPromptTokens
			lastFinishReason = sr.FinishReason
			lastHttpStatus = sr.HttpStatus
			history = append(history, ChatMessage{Role: "assistant", Content: sr.OutputText})
		}
		itlAvg = itlSum / 10.0
	}

	if totalPromptTokens <= 0 || totalCompletionTokens <= 0 {
		panic(fmt.Sprintf("FATAL: Validation failed - prompt_tokens (%d) <= 0 or completion_tokens (%d) <= 0", totalPromptTokens, totalCompletionTokens))
	}

	tTaskMs := float64(time.Since(tStart).Nanoseconds()) / 1e6
	tHttpMs := math.Max(0.0, tTaskMs-tNeuralTotal-tDispatchTotal)
	tOrchMs := math.Max(0.0, tTaskMs-tNeuralTotal)
	phiOrch := 0.0
	if tTaskMs > 0 {
		phiOrch = (tOrchMs / tTaskMs) * 100.0
	}
	rTool := 0.0
	if toolCalls > 0 && tDispatchTotal > 0 {
		rTool = float64(toolCalls) / (tDispatchTotal / 1000.0)
	}
	rssPeak := getRSSMB()
	if rssPeak < rssIdle {
		rssPeak = rssIdle
	}

	cacheHitPct := 0.0
	if totalPromptTokens > 0 {
		cacheHitPct = (float64(totalCachedTokens) / float64(totalPromptTokens)) * 100.0
	}

	return TrialResult{
		Architecture:         "PicoClaw (Go 1.23)",
		Workload:             tier,
		Warmth:               warmth,
		TTaskMs:              tTaskMs,
		TNeuralMs:            tNeuralTotal,
		TOrchMs:              tOrchMs,
		PhiOrchPct:           phiOrch,
		TTFTMs:               ttftFirst,
		ITLMs:                itlAvg,
		TDispatchMs:          tDispatchTotal,
		THttpMs:              tHttpMs,
		RToolOpsSec:          rTool,
		RSSIdleMB:            rssIdle,
		RSSPeakMB:            rssPeak,
		AlphaMem:             rssPeak / math.Max(1.0, rssIdle),
		PromptTokens:         totalPromptTokens,
		CompletionTokens:     totalCompletionTokens,
		OutputTokens:         totalCompletionTokens,
		CachedTokens:         totalCachedTokens,
		UncachedPromptTokens: totalUncachedPromptTokens,
		CacheHitPct:          cacheHitPct,
		ToolCalls:            toolCalls,
		HttpStatus:           lastHttpStatus,
		FinishReason:         lastFinishReason,
	}
}

func main() {
	tier := "W1"
	warmth := "Cold"
	reps := 1
	apiURL := "http://127.0.0.1:8080/v1/chat/completions"
	if envURL := os.Getenv("LLAMA_API_URL"); envURL != "" {
		apiURL = envURL
	}
	baseDir := os.Getenv("WORKLOAD_DATA_DIR")
	if baseDir == "" {
		candidates := []string{
			"./data/workload_data",
			"../data/workload_data",
		}
		for _, c := range candidates {
			if _, err := os.Stat(c); err == nil {
				baseDir = c
				break
			}
		}
	}
	if baseDir == "" {
		baseDir = "./data/workload_data"
	}
	if len(os.Args) > 1 {
		tier = os.Args[1]
	}
	if len(os.Args) > 2 {
		warmth = os.Args[2]
	}
	if len(os.Args) > 3 {
		fmt.Sscanf(os.Args[3], "%d", &reps)
	}
	rssIdle := getRSSMB()
	client := &http.Client{Timeout: 120 * time.Second}
	var results []TrialResult
	for i := 0; i < reps; i++ {
		res := runWorkload(client, apiURL, baseDir, tier, warmth, rssIdle)
		results = append(results, res)
	}
	out, _ := json.Marshal(results)
	fmt.Println(string(out))
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * OpenClaw TypeScript/Node.js Orchestrator Benchmark Worker
 * Evaluates V8 dynamic module loading, JSON schema validation, object allocation,
 * generational GC pressure, and tool dispatch under equalized neural workloads.
 */
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { performance } from "node:perf_hooks";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import v8 from "node:v8";

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);
const REPO_DIR = path.resolve(__dirname, "..");
const API_URL = process.env.LLAMA_API_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:8080/v1/chat/completions";
const BASE_DIR = process.env.WORKLOAD_DATA_DIR || path.join(REPO_DIR, "data", "workload_data");

function getRSSMB() {
  return process.memoryUsage().rss / (1024.0 * 1024.0);
}

// Build OpenClaw's tool schema catalog & prompt context builder
// Supports ablation modes: "full" (54 tools stock enterprise) vs "minimal" (15 tools equalized)
function buildOpenClawToolCatalog() {
  const mode = process.env.OPENCLAW_MODE || "minimal";
  const toolCount = mode === "full" ? 54 : 15;
  const catalog = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < toolCount; i++) {
    catalog.push({
      type: "function",
      function: {
        name: `system_tool_${i}`,
        description: `Standard system tool descriptor #${i} for agent runtime evaluation.`,
        parameters: {
          type: "object",
          properties: {
            action: { type: "string", description: "Action to execute" },
            target: { type: "string", description: "Target parameter or file path" }
          },
          required: ["action", "target"]
        }
      }
    });
  }
  // V8 object allocation and schema serialization pass
  const serialized = JSON.stringify(catalog);
  return JSON.parse(serialized);
}

function executeToolListDir(mockDir) {
  const entries = fs.readdirSync(mockDir);
  const files = entries.map((name) => {
    try {
      const st = fs.statSync(path.join(mockDir, name));
      return `${name}:${st.size}`;
    } catch {
      return `${name}:0`;
    }
  });
  return JSON.stringify({ count: files.length, files: files.slice(0, 10) });
}

function executeToolGrepLogs(logFile) {
  const raw = fs.readFileSync(logFile, "utf-8");
  const lines = raw.split("\n");
  const re = /"subsystem":\s*"UMA_BUS_CONTROLLER"/;
  const vals = [];
  for (const line of lines) {
    if (re.test(line)) {
      try {
        const obj = JSON.parse(line);
        if (typeof obj.latency_ms === "number") {
          vals.push(obj.latency_ms);
        }
      } catch {}
    }
  }
  return {
    vals,
    summary: JSON.stringify({ matched_records: vals.length, sample: vals.slice(0, 5) })
  };
}

function executeToolCalcStats(vals) {
  const n = vals.length;
  if (n === 0) return "{}";
  const mean = vals.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n;
  let variance = 0.0;
  if (n > 1) {
    variance = vals.reduce((a, v) => a + (v - mean) ** 2, 0) / (n - 1);
  }
  return JSON.stringify({ n, mean, variance, stddev: Math.sqrt(variance) });
}

function executeVectorRAG(baseDir) {
  const inDim = 256;
  const outDim = 384;
  const nDocs = 10000;

  const wFile = path.join(baseDir, "w_proj.bin");
  const bFile = path.join(baseDir, "b_proj.bin");
  const docsBinFile = path.join(baseDir, "doc_embeddings.bin");

  const wBuf = fs.readFileSync(wFile);
  const bBuf = fs.readFileSync(bFile);
  const docBuf = fs.readFileSync(docsBinFile);

  if (wBuf.length !== inDim * outDim * 4 || bBuf.length !== outDim * 4 || docBuf.length !== nDocs * outDim * 4) {
    throw new Error(`FATAL: Required RAG binary datasets corrupted or incomplete in ${baseDir}`);
  }

  const wProj = new Float32Array(wBuf.buffer, wBuf.byteOffset, inDim * outDim);
  const bProj = new Float32Array(bBuf.buffer, bBuf.byteOffset, outDim);
  const docMatrix = new Float32Array(docBuf.buffer, docBuf.byteOffset, nDocs * outDim);

  // 1. Query vector initialization and linear projection (256 -> 384)
  const qRaw = new Float32Array(inDim);
  for (let d = 0; d < inDim; d++) {
    qRaw[d] = Math.sin(d * 0.17) * 0.05;
  }

  const qProj = new Float32Array(outDim);
  for (let j = 0; j < outDim; j++) {
    let sum = bProj[j];
    for (let i = 0; i < inDim; i++) {
      sum += qRaw[i] * wProj[i * outDim + j];
    }
    qProj[j] = sum;
  }

  // 2. L2 Normalization of projected query vector
  let normSq = 0.0;
  for (let j = 0; j < outDim; j++) {
    normSq += qProj[j] * qProj[j];
  }
  const norm = Math.sqrt(normSq) + 1e-8;
  for (let j = 0; j < outDim; j++) {
    qProj[j] /= norm;
  }

  // 3. Cosine similarity against all 10,000 document vectors
  const scores = new Array(nDocs);
  for (let i = 0; i < nDocs; i++) {
    let dot = 0.0;
    const rowOffset = i * outDim;
    for (let j = 0; j < outDim; j++) {
      dot += qProj[j] * docMatrix[rowOffset + j];
    }
    scores[i] = { idx: i, score: dot };
  }

  // 4. Descending sort to select Top-5 chunks
  scores.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);

  const chunksRaw = fs.readFileSync(path.join(baseDir, "rag_chunks_10k.json"), "utf-8");
  const chunks = JSON.parse(chunksRaw);

  let out = "";
  for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) {
    const topIdx = scores[k].idx;
    out += (chunks[String(topIdx)] || `[Chunk #${topIdx}] Context reference document.`) + "\n";
  }
  return out;
}

async function callLlamaStream(messages, tools, maxTokens) {
  const payload = JSON.stringify({
    model: "Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct-Q4_K_M.gguf",
    messages,
    tools,
    max_tokens: maxTokens,
    temperature: 0.0,
    seed: 42,
    stream: true,
    cache_prompt: process.env.BENCH_CACHE_PROMPT === "true"
  });
  const t0 = performance.now();
  const resp = await fetch(API_URL, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: payload
  });

  if (resp.status !== 200) {
    const errText = await resp.text();
    throw new Error(`FATAL: llama-server returned HTTP ${resp.status}: ${errText}`);
  }

  let ttftMs = 0.0;
  let promptMs = 0.0;
  let predictedMs = 0.0;
  let hasServerTimings = false;
  let firstChunk = true;
  let tokenCount = 0;
  let cachedTokens = 0;
  let uncachedPromptTokens = 0;
  let usagePromptTokens = 0;
  let completionTokens = 0;
  let finishReason = "";
  let outText = "";
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  let buf = "";

  let isDone = false;
  for await (const chunk of resp.body) {
    if (isDone) break;
    buf += decoder.decode(chunk, { stream: true });
    const lines = buf.split("\n");
    buf = lines.pop() || "";
    for (let line of lines) {
      line = line.trim();
      if (line.startsWith("data: ")) {
        const dataStr = line.slice(6);
        if (dataStr === "[DONE]") {
          isDone = true;
          break;
        }
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(dataStr);
          if (parsed.timings) {
            if (typeof parsed.timings.prompt_ms === "number") {
              promptMs = parsed.timings.prompt_ms;
              hasServerTimings = true;
            }
            if (typeof parsed.timings.predicted_ms === "number") {
              predictedMs = parsed.timings.predicted_ms;
              hasServerTimings = true;
            }
            if (typeof parsed.timings.cache_n === "number" && parsed.timings.cache_n >= 0) {
              cachedTokens = parsed.timings.cache_n;
            }
            if (typeof parsed.timings.prompt_n === "number" && parsed.timings.prompt_n >= 0) {
              uncachedPromptTokens = parsed.timings.prompt_n;
            }
            if (typeof parsed.timings.predicted_n === "number" && parsed.timings.predicted_n > 0) {
              completionTokens = parsed.timings.predicted_n;
            }
          }
          if (parsed.usage) {
            if (typeof parsed.usage.prompt_tokens === "number" && parsed.usage.prompt_tokens > 0) {
              usagePromptTokens = parsed.usage.prompt_tokens;
            }
            if (typeof parsed.usage.completion_tokens === "number" && parsed.usage.completion_tokens > 0) {
              completionTokens = parsed.usage.completion_tokens;
            }
          }
          const c0 = parsed.choices?.[0];
          if (c0) {
            if (c0.finish_reason && typeof c0.finish_reason === "string") {
              finishReason = c0.finish_reason;
            }
            const delta = c0.delta;
            if (delta) {
              let hasData = false;
              if (delta.content && delta.content.length > 0) {
                hasData = true;
                tokenCount++;
                outText += delta.content;
              }
              if (delta.tool_calls && delta.tool_calls.length > 0) {
                hasData = true;
                tokenCount += delta.tool_calls.length;
              }
              if (hasData && firstChunk) {
                ttftMs = performance.now() - t0;
                firstChunk = false;
              }
            }
          }
        } catch {}
      }
    }
  }

  const promptTokens = usagePromptTokens > 0 ? usagePromptTokens : (cachedTokens + uncachedPromptTokens);
  const totalNeuralMs = hasServerTimings ? (promptMs + predictedMs) : (performance.now() - t0);
  if (completionTokens === 0) completionTokens = tokenCount;
  if (!finishReason) finishReason = "stop";
  const itlMs = completionTokens > 1 ? (totalNeuralMs - ttftMs) / (completionTokens - 1) : 0.0;
  return {
    totalNeuralMs,
    ttftMs,
    itlMs,
    promptTokens,
    completionTokens,
    cachedTokens,
    uncachedPromptTokens,
    finishReason,
    httpStatus: resp.status,
    outText
  };
}

async function runWorkload(tier, warmth, rssIdle) {
  const tStart = performance.now();
  let tNeuralTotal = 0.0;
  let ttftFirst = 0.0;
  let itlAvg = 0.0;
  let tDispatchTotal = 0.0;
  let totalPromptTokens = 0;
  let totalCompletionTokens = 0;
  let totalCachedTokens = 0;
  let totalUncachedPromptTokens = 0;
  let toolCalls = 0;
  let lastFinishReason = "";
  let lastHttpStatus = 200;

  const tCatalog0 = performance.now();
  const toolCatalog = buildOpenClawToolCatalog();
  const tCatalogMs = performance.now() - tCatalog0;

  const mockDir = path.join(BASE_DIR, "mock_files");
  const logFile = path.join(BASE_DIR, "system_logs.jsonl");

  if (tier === "W1") {
    tDispatchTotal = tCatalogMs;
    const prompt =
      "Analyze Apple Silicon M4 Unified Memory Architecture cache coherence and LPDDR5X bandwidth allocation. ".repeat(8) +
      "Summarize the primary latency determinant in two concise sentences.";
    const msgs = [
      { role: "system", content: "You are a concise technical systems assistant." },
      { role: "user", content: prompt }
    ];
    const r = await callLlamaStream(msgs, toolCatalog, 64);
    tNeuralTotal = r.totalNeuralMs;
    ttftFirst = r.ttftMs;
    itlAvg = r.itlMs;
    totalPromptTokens = r.promptTokens;
    totalCompletionTokens = r.completionTokens;
    totalCachedTokens = r.cachedTokens;
    totalUncachedPromptTokens = r.uncachedPromptTokens;
    lastFinishReason = r.finishReason;
    lastHttpStatus = r.httpStatus;
    toolCalls = 0;
  } else if (tier === "W2") {
    const tTool0 = performance.now();
    const res1 = executeToolListDir(mockDir);
    const { vals, summary: res2 } = executeToolGrepLogs(logFile);
    const res3 = executeToolCalcStats(vals);
    tDispatchTotal = tCatalogMs + (performance.now() - tTool0);
    toolCalls = 3;

    const prompt = `System Tool Chain Results:\n1. list_dir: ${res1}\n2. grep_logs: ${res2}\n3. calc_stats: ${res3}\nEvaluate the bus latency metrics and execute the next diagnostic step.`;
    const msgs = [
      { role: "system", content: "You are a concise technical systems assistant." },
      { role: "user", content: prompt }
    ];
    const r = await callLlamaStream(msgs, toolCatalog, 128);
    tNeuralTotal = r.totalNeuralMs;
    ttftFirst = r.ttftMs;
    itlAvg = r.itlMs;
    totalPromptTokens = r.promptTokens;
    totalCompletionTokens = r.completionTokens;
    totalCachedTokens = r.cachedTokens;
    totalUncachedPromptTokens = r.uncachedPromptTokens;
    lastFinishReason = r.finishReason;
    lastHttpStatus = r.httpStatus;
  } else if (tier === "W3") {
    const tTool0 = performance.now();
    const ragContext = executeVectorRAG(BASE_DIR);
    tDispatchTotal = tCatalogMs + (performance.now() - tTool0);
    toolCalls = 1;

    const msgs = [
      { role: "system", content: "You are a concise technical systems assistant." },
      { role: "user", content: "Retrieved Context:\n" + ragContext + "\nQuestion: How do UMA bandwidth and runtime GC interact during local agent execution?" }
    ];
    const r = await callLlamaStream(msgs, toolCatalog, 256);
    tNeuralTotal = r.totalNeuralMs;
    ttftFirst = r.ttftMs;
    itlAvg = r.itlMs;
    totalPromptTokens = r.promptTokens;
    totalCompletionTokens = r.completionTokens;
    totalCachedTokens = r.cachedTokens;
    totalUncachedPromptTokens = r.uncachedPromptTokens;
    lastFinishReason = r.finishReason;
    lastHttpStatus = r.httpStatus;
  } else if (tier === "W4") {
    toolCalls = 10;
    let itlSum = 0.0;
    const history = [
      { role: "system", content: "You are a concise technical systems assistant." }
    ];
    tDispatchTotal = tCatalogMs;
    for (let step = 1; step <= 10; step++) {
      const tTool0 = performance.now();
      executeToolListDir(mockDir);
      const { vals } = executeToolGrepLogs(logFile);
      const statStr = executeToolCalcStats(vals);
      tDispatchTotal += (performance.now() - tTool0);

      history.push({
        role: "user",
        content: `Turn ${step} Observation: ${statStr}. State next brief diagnostic action.`
      });
      const r = await callLlamaStream(history, toolCatalog, 64);
      tNeuralTotal += r.totalNeuralMs;
      if (step === 1) ttftFirst = r.ttftMs;
      itlSum += r.itlMs;
      totalPromptTokens += r.promptTokens;
      totalCompletionTokens += r.completionTokens;
      totalCachedTokens += r.cachedTokens;
      totalUncachedPromptTokens += r.uncachedPromptTokens;
      lastFinishReason = r.finishReason;
      lastHttpStatus = r.httpStatus;
      history.push({ role: "assistant", content: r.outText });
    }
    itlAvg = itlSum / 10.0;
  }

  if (totalPromptTokens <= 0 || totalCompletionTokens <= 0) {
    throw new Error(`FATAL: Validation failed - prompt_tokens (${totalPromptTokens}) <= 0 or completion_tokens (${totalCompletionTokens}) <= 0`);
  }

  const tTaskMs = performance.now() - tStart;
  const tHttpMs = Math.max(0.0, tTaskMs - tNeuralTotal - tDispatchTotal);
  const tOrchMs = Math.max(0.0, tTaskMs - tNeuralTotal);
  const phiOrch = tTaskMs > 0 ? (tOrchMs / tTaskMs) * 100.0 : 0.0;
  const rTool = toolCalls > 0 && tDispatchTotal > 0 ? toolCalls / (tDispatchTotal / 1000.0) : 0.0;
  const rssPeak = Math.max(rssIdle, getRSSMB());
  const alphaMem = rssPeak / Math.max(1.0, rssIdle);
  const v8UsedHeapMb = v8.getHeapStatistics().used_heap_size / (1024.0 * 1024.0);
  const mode = process.env.OPENCLAW_MODE || "minimal";
  const cacheHitPct = totalPromptTokens > 0 ? (totalCachedTokens / totalPromptTokens) * 100.0 : 0.0;

  return {
    architecture: `OpenClaw [${mode}] (Node.js v26.12)`,
    workload: tier,
    warmth: warmth,
    t_task_ms: tTaskMs,
    t_neural_ms: tNeuralTotal,
    t_orch_ms: tOrchMs,
    phi_orch_pct: phiOrch,
    ttft_ms: ttftFirst,
    itl_ms: itlAvg,
    t_dispatch_ms: tDispatchTotal,
    t_http_ms: tHttpMs,
    r_tool_ops_sec: rTool,
    rss_idle_mb: rssIdle,
    rss_peak_mb: rssPeak,
    v8_used_heap_mb: v8UsedHeapMb,
    alpha_mem: alphaMem,
    prompt_tokens: totalPromptTokens,
    completion_tokens: totalCompletionTokens,
    output_tokens: totalCompletionTokens,
    cached_tokens: totalCachedTokens,
    uncached_prompt_tokens: totalUncachedPromptTokens,
    cache_hit_pct: cacheHitPct,
    tool_calls: toolCalls,
    http_status: lastHttpStatus,
    finish_reason: lastFinishReason
  };
}

async function main() {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  const tier = args[0] || "W1";
  const warmth = args[1] || "Cold";
  const reps = parseInt(args[2] || "1", 10);
  const rssIdle = getRSSMB();
  const results = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < reps; i++) {
    const res = await runWorkload(tier, warmth, rssIdle);
    results.push(res);
  }
  console.log(JSON.stringify(results));
}

main().catch((err) => {
  console.error("OpenClaw Worker Error:", err);
  process.exit(1);
});
