"""
benchmark/analyze_results.py - Rigorous Non-Parametric Statistical Analysis
Calculates median (p50), interquartile range (IQR), Mann-Whitney U tests with
Holm-Bonferroni correction, Cliff's Delta, Vargha-Delaney A12 effect sizes with
Bootstrap 95% Confidence Intervals (2,000 resamples), and produces publication tables.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import sys
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
from scipy import stats

REPO_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))


def cliffs_delta(lst1: list[float], lst2: list[float]) -> float:
    """
    Compute Cliff's Delta non-parametric effect size d in [-1, 1].
    - d = +1.0: lst1 is strictly greater than lst2 in all pairwise comparisons.
    - d = -1.0: lst1 is strictly smaller than lst2 in all pairwise comparisons.
    - d = 0.0: No stochastic dominance.
    """
    n1, n2 = len(lst1), len(lst2)
    if n1 == 0 or n2 == 0:
        return 0.0
    more = sum(1 for x in lst1 for y in lst2 if x > y)
    less = sum(1 for x in lst1 for y in lst2 if x < y)
    return (more - less) / float(n1 * n2)


def vargha_delaney_a12(lst1: list[float], lst2: list[float]) -> float:
    """
    Compute Vargha-Delaney A12 effect size statistic in [0, 1].
    A12 = P(X > Y) + 0.5 * P(X == Y) = (d + 1) / 2.
    """
    d = cliffs_delta(lst1, lst2)
    return (d + 1.0) / 2.0


def bootstrap_ci(
    data: list[float],
    n_resamples: int = 2000,
    alpha: float = 0.05,
    seed: int = 42,
) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """
    Computes empirical 95% bootstrap confidence interval for the sample median.
    """
    if len(data) == 0:
        return 0.0, 0.0
    if len(data) == 1:
        return float(data[0]), float(data[0])

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    arr = np.array(data, dtype=np.float64)
    boot_medians = np.empty(n_resamples, dtype=np.float64)

    for i in range(n_resamples):
        sample = rng.choice(arr, size=len(arr), replace=True)
        boot_medians[i] = np.median(sample)

    lower = float(np.percentile(boot_medians, 100.0 * (alpha / 2.0)))
    upper = float(np.percentile(boot_medians, 100.0 * (1.0 - alpha / 2.0)))
    return round(lower, 4), round(upper, 4)


def holm_bonferroni_adjust(p_values: list[float]) -> list[float]:
    """
    Adjusts a list of p-values using the step-down Holm-Bonferroni method.
    Guarantees strong family-wise error rate control under multiple comparisons.
    """
    m = len(p_values)
    if m == 0:
        return []

    # Sort indices by original p-value
    indexed = sorted(enumerate(p_values), key=lambda x: x[1])
    adjusted = [0.0] * m
    running_max = 0.0

    for rank, (orig_idx, p_val) in enumerate(indexed):
        # Step-down multiplier: (m - rank)
        adj_p = p_val * (m - rank)
        adj_p = min(1.0, max(running_max, adj_p))
        running_max = adj_p
        adjusted[orig_idx] = round(adj_p, 6)

    return adjusted


def analyze_publication_campaign(input_path: str | None = None, output_dir: str | None = None):
    if input_path is None:
        input_path = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "results", "publication_campaign", "raw_trials.jsonl")
    if output_dir is None:
        output_dir = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "results", "publication_campaign")

    os.makedirs(os.path.join(output_dir, "tables"), exist_ok=True)

    if not os.path.exists(input_path):
        print(f"Error: {input_path} not found. Please run benchmark/run_unified_benchmark.py first.")
        return

    records = []
    with open(input_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for line in f:
            if line.strip():
                try:
                    records.append(json.loads(line))
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # Filter out warmup runs if marked
    eval_records = [r for r in records if not r.get("is_warmup", False)]
    if not eval_records:
        eval_records = records

    # Audit and filter invalid/failed trials
    valid_records = []
    for r in eval_records:
        status = r.get("http_status", 200)
        fr = r.get("finish_reason", "stop")
        if status != 200:
            print(f"⚠️  [WARNING] Dropping trial with non-200 HTTP status: {status}")
            continue
        if fr not in ("stop", "length", "tool_calls"):
            print(f"⚠️  [NOTICE] Unconventional finish_reason in trial: {fr}")
        pt = r.get("prompt_tokens", 0)
        ct = r.get("completion_tokens") or r.get("output_tokens", 0)
        if pt is None or int(pt) <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                f"FATAL: Evaluated trial record {r.get('agent_key')} {r.get('workload')} Rep {r.get('rep')} "
                f"registered invalid prompt_tokens <= 0 ({pt}). All evaluated trials must record positive prompt tokens."
            )
        if ct is None or int(ct) <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                f"FATAL: Evaluated trial record {r.get('agent_key')} {r.get('workload')} Rep {r.get('rep')} "
                f"registered invalid completion_tokens <= 0 ({ct}). All evaluated trials must record positive completion tokens."
            )
        valid_records.append(r)
    eval_records = valid_records

    print(f"Loaded {len(eval_records)} evaluated trial records (filtered {len(records) - len(eval_records)} warmups).")

    # Group by (agent_key, workload)
    groups: dict[str, dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]] = {}
    for r in eval_records:
        ak = r.get("agent_key") or r.get("architecture", "unknown")
        w = r.get("workload", "W1")
        groups.setdefault(ak, {}).setdefault(w, []).append(r)

    summary_stats: dict[str, Any] = {}
    csv_rows = [
        "agent,workload,n,t_task_median_ms,t_task_iqr_ms,t_task_mean_ms,t_task_std_ms,t_task_p95_ms,"
        "t_orch_median_ms,rss_peak_mb,energy_total_mean_j,energy_net_mean_j,edp_mean_js,joules_per_token"
    ]

    for ak, w_dict in groups.items():
        summary_stats[ak] = {}
        for w, items in w_dict.items():
            for item in items:
                pt_val = item.get("prompt_tokens", 0)
                ct_val = item.get("completion_tokens") or item.get("output_tokens", 0)
                assert pt_val is not None and int(pt_val) > 0, f"Invariant violation: prompt_tokens <= 0 in {ak} {w}"
                assert ct_val is not None and int(ct_val) > 0, f"Invariant violation: completion_tokens <= 0 in {ak} {w}"

            t_tasks = [float(x["t_task_ms"]) for x in items if x.get("t_task_ms") is not None]
            t_orchs = [float(x["t_orch_ms"]) for x in items if x.get("t_orch_ms") is not None]
            t_dispatches = [float(x.get("t_dispatch_ms", 0.0)) for x in items if x.get("t_dispatch_ms") is not None]
            rss_peaks = [float(x["rss_peak_mb"]) for x in items if x.get("rss_peak_mb") is not None]
            proc_phys = [float(x.get("proc_peak_phys_mb", 0.0)) for x in items if x.get("proc_peak_phys_mb") is not None]
            proc_inst = [float(x.get("proc_instructions", 0.0)) for x in items if x.get("proc_instructions") is not None]
            proc_cyc = [float(x.get("proc_cycles", 0.0)) for x in items if x.get("proc_cycles") is not None]
            proc_billed = [float(x.get("proc_billed_energy", 0.0)) for x in items if x.get("proc_billed_energy") is not None]
            pts = [int(x.get("prompt_tokens", 0)) for x in items]
            cts = [int(x.get("completion_tokens") or x.get("output_tokens", 0)) for x in items]
            frs = [str(x.get("finish_reason", "stop")) for x in items]

            # Collect energy metrics (handling None for unprivileged runs)
            energies_total = [float(x["energy_total_j"]) for x in items if x.get("energy_total_j") is not None]
            energies_net = [float(x["energy_net_above_idle_j"]) for x in items if x.get("energy_net_above_idle_j") is not None]
            edps_net = [
                float(x["energy_net_above_idle_j"]) * (float(x["t_task_ms"]) / 1000.0)
                for x in items
                if x.get("energy_net_above_idle_j") is not None and x.get("t_task_ms") is not None
            ]
            j_tokens_net = [
                float(x["energy_net_above_idle_j"]) / float(x.get("completion_tokens") or x.get("output_tokens") or 1)
                for x in items
                if x.get("energy_net_above_idle_j") is not None and (x.get("completion_tokens") or x.get("output_tokens", 0)) > 0
            ]

            # Primary non-parametric metrics
            p50_t = float(np.percentile(t_tasks, 50)) if t_tasks else 0.0
            q25_t = float(np.percentile(t_tasks, 25)) if t_tasks else 0.0
            q75_t = float(np.percentile(t_tasks, 75)) if t_tasks else 0.0
            iqr_t = q75_t - q25_t
            p95_t = float(np.percentile(t_tasks, 95)) if t_tasks else 0.0
            mean_t = float(np.mean(t_tasks)) if t_tasks else 0.0
            std_t = float(np.std(t_tasks, ddof=1)) if len(t_tasks) > 1 else 0.0

            p50_orch = float(np.percentile(t_orchs, 50)) if t_orchs else 0.0
            mean_orch = float(np.mean(t_orchs)) if t_orchs else 0.0
            p50_disp = float(np.percentile(t_dispatches, 50)) if t_dispatches else 0.0
            mean_rss = float(np.mean(rss_peaks)) if rss_peaks else 0.0
            mean_phys = float(np.mean(proc_phys)) if proc_phys else mean_rss
            mean_inst = int(np.mean(proc_inst)) if proc_inst else 0
            mean_cyc = int(np.mean(proc_cyc)) if proc_cyc else 0
            mean_billed = int(np.mean(proc_billed)) if proc_billed else 0

            mean_e_total = float(np.mean(energies_total)) if energies_total else None
            mean_e_net = float(np.mean(energies_net)) if energies_net else None
            mean_edp = float(np.mean(edps_net)) if edps_net else None
            mean_jtok = float(np.mean(j_tokens_net)) if j_tokens_net else None

            ci_low, ci_high = bootstrap_ci(t_tasks)

            summary_stats[ak][w] = {
                "sample_count": len(t_tasks),
                "t_task_median_ms": round(p50_t, 2),
                "t_task_iqr_ms": round(iqr_t, 2),
                "t_task_bootstrap_ci_95": [ci_low, ci_high],
                "t_task_mean_ms": round(mean_t, 2),
                "t_task_std_ms": round(std_t, 2),
                "t_task_p95_ms": round(p95_t, 2),
                "t_orch_median_ms": round(p50_orch, 3),
                "t_orch_mean_ms": round(mean_orch, 3),
                "t_dispatch_median_ms": round(p50_disp, 3),
                "rss_peak_mb": round(mean_rss, 2),
                "proc_peak_phys_mb": round(mean_phys, 2),
                "proc_instructions": mean_inst,
                "proc_cycles": mean_cyc,
                "proc_billed_energy": mean_billed,
                "prompt_tokens_mean": round(float(np.mean(pts)), 2) if pts else 0,
                "completion_tokens_mean": round(float(np.mean(cts)), 2) if cts else 0,
                "finish_reason": frs[0] if frs else "stop",
                "energy_total_mean_j": round(mean_e_total, 3) if mean_e_total is not None else None,
                "energy_net_mean_j": round(mean_e_net, 3) if mean_e_net is not None else None,
                "edp_mean_js": round(mean_edp, 3) if mean_edp is not None else None,
                "joules_per_token": round(mean_jtok, 4) if mean_jtok is not None else None,
            }

            e_str = f"{mean_e_total:.3f}" if mean_e_total is not None else "N/A"
            enet_str = f"{mean_e_net:.3f}" if mean_e_net is not None else "N/A"
            edp_str = f"{mean_edp:.3f}" if mean_edp is not None else "N/A"
            jtok_str = f"{mean_jtok:.4f}" if mean_jtok is not None else "N/A"

            csv_rows.append(
                f"{ak},{w},{len(t_tasks)},{p50_t:.2f},{iqr_t:.2f},{mean_t:.2f},{std_t:.2f},{p95_t:.2f},"
                f"{p50_orch:.3f},{mean_rss:.2f},{e_str},{enet_str},{edp_str},{jtok_str}"
            )

    # Statistical comparisons with Holm-Bonferroni correction
    comparisons: dict[str, Any] = {}
    pairs = [
        ("picoclaw", "openclaw_minimal", "PicoClaw_vs_OpenClawMinimal"),
        ("picoclaw", "openclaw_full", "PicoClaw_vs_OpenClawFull"),
        ("picoclaw", "python_native", "PicoClaw_vs_PythonNative"),
        ("openclaw_minimal", "openclaw_full", "OpenClawAblation_Minimal_vs_Full"),
        ("neural_baseline", "picoclaw", "NeuralBaseline_vs_PicoClaw"),
    ]

    all_p_entries = []
    for a1, a2, label in pairs:
        comparisons[label] = {}
        for w in ["W1", "W2", "W3", "W4"]:
            vals1 = [float(x["t_task_ms"]) for x in groups.get(a1, {}).get(w, []) if x.get("t_task_ms") is not None]
            vals2 = [float(x["t_task_ms"]) for x in groups.get(a2, {}).get(w, []) if x.get("t_task_ms") is not None]
            if len(vals1) > 0 and len(vals2) > 0:
                try:
                    u_stat, raw_p = stats.mannwhitneyu(vals1, vals2, alternative="two-sided")
                except Exception:
                    u_stat, raw_p = 0.0, 1.0

                d = cliffs_delta(vals1, vals2)
                a12 = vargha_delaney_a12(vals1, vals2)

                entry = {
                    "u_statistic": float(u_stat),
                    "raw_p_value": float(raw_p),
                    "cliffs_delta": round(float(d), 4),
                    "vargha_delaney_a12": round(float(a12), 4),
                }
                comparisons[label][w] = entry
                all_p_entries.append((label, w, raw_p))

    # Apply Holm-Bonferroni correction across all hypotheses
    if all_p_entries:
        raw_p_list = [item[2] for item in all_p_entries]
        adj_p_list = holm_bonferroni_adjust(raw_p_list)
        for idx, (lbl, w, _) in enumerate(all_p_entries):
            adj_p = adj_p_list[idx]
            comparisons[lbl][w]["holm_bonferroni_p_value"] = adj_p
            comparisons[lbl][w]["significant_alpha_0_05"] = bool(adj_p < 0.05)

    # Save JSON summary & statistical tests
    stats_out = os.path.join(output_dir, "summary_statistics.json")
    with open(stats_out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(summary_stats, f, indent=2)

    tests_out = os.path.join(output_dir, "statistical_tests.json")
    with open(tests_out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(comparisons, f, indent=2)

    # Save CSV
    csv_out = os.path.join(output_dir, "tables", "metrics_summary.csv")
    with open(csv_out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("\n".join(csv_rows) + "\n")

    # Generate LaTeX Table (table_journal_ablation.tex) with complete 9 columns and dynamic footer metadata
    latex_table = r"""\begin{table*}[t]
\centering
\caption{Empirical Performance Evaluation and Runtime Ablation Matrix on Apple Silicon M4. Peak Phys RAM denotes lifetime maximum resident physical footprint recorded via Darwin kernel \texttt{libproc}. Net Energy is integrated above quiescent idle baseline via Apple SMC hardware shunts at 50~ms resolution. Dispatch and HTTP latency isolate tool-execution and network overhead ($T_{\text{orch}} \approx T_{\text{dispatch}} + T_{\text{http}}$). Prompt and Completion token counts reflect tokenizer throughput.}
\label{tab:evaluation_ablation_m4}
\small
\begin{tabular}{l l c r r r r r r}
\hline
\textbf{Workload} & \textbf{Configuration} & \textbf{Prompt/Comp} & \textbf{Median (IQR) [ms]} & \textbf{Disp / HTTP [ms]} & \textbf{Peak Phys [MB]} & \textbf{Net Energy [J]} & \textbf{EDP [J$\cdot$s]} & \textbf{Billed / Cycles} \\
\hline
"""
    agents_order = [
        ("neural_baseline", "Neural Baseline (Direct)"),
        ("picoclaw", "PicoClaw (Go Native)"),
        ("openclaw_minimal", "OpenClaw (Node.js Minimal)"),
        ("openclaw_full", "OpenClaw (Node.js Full 54-Tool)"),
        ("python_native", "PythonNative (CPython Reference)"),
    ]

    for w in ["W1", "W2", "W3", "W4"]:
        for ak, label in agents_order:
            entry = summary_stats.get(ak, {}).get(w)
            if entry:
                items = groups.get(ak, {}).get(w, [])
                p_tok = int(round(np.mean([float(x.get("prompt_tokens", 0)) for x in items]))) if items else 0
                c_tok = int(round(np.mean([float(x.get("completion_tokens") or x.get("output_tokens", 0)) for x in items]))) if items else 0
                p50 = entry["t_task_median_ms"]
                iqr = entry["t_task_iqr_ms"]
                d_m = entry.get("t_dispatch_median_ms", 0.0)
                h_m = float(np.percentile([float(x.get("t_http_ms", 0.0)) for x in items if x.get("t_http_ms") is not None], 50)) if items else 0.0
                disp_http = f"{d_m:.2f} / {h_m:.2f}"
                ram = entry.get("proc_peak_phys_mb", entry["rss_peak_mb"])
                enet_val = entry.get("energy_net_mean_j")
                enet_str = f"{enet_val:.2f}" if enet_val is not None else "N/A"
                edp_val = entry.get("edp_mean_js")
                edp_str = f"{edp_val:.2f}" if edp_val is not None else "N/A"
                billed = entry.get("proc_billed_energy", 0)
                cyc = entry.get("proc_cycles", 0)
                cyc_str = f"{cyc/1e6:.1f}M" if cyc > 0 else "0"
                billed_cyc_str = f"{billed} / {cyc_str}" if billed > 0 or cyc > 0 else "N/A"
                latex_table += f"{w} & {label} & {p_tok} / {c_tok} & {p50:.1f} ({iqr:.1f}) & {disp_http} & {ram:.1f} & {enet_str} & {edp_str} & {billed_cyc_str} \\\\\n"
        latex_table += r"\hline" + "\n"

    total_evals = len(eval_records)
    warmups_count = len(records) - len(eval_records)
    cond_count = len(groups) * 4 if groups else 1
    n_per_cond = int(total_evals / cond_count) if cond_count > 0 else 0
    backends = sorted(list({str(r.get("telemetry_backend", "apple_smc_powermetrics")) for r in eval_records}))
    backend_str = "Apple Silicon SMC (\\texttt{powermetrics} 50~ms current shunt sampling) + Darwin XNU \\texttt{libproc}" if "apple_smc_powermetrics" in backends else ", ".join(backends)
    datasources = sorted(list({str(r.get("data_source", "measured_hardware")) for r in eval_records}))
    datasource_str = ", ".join(datasources).replace("_", r"\_")

    latex_table += f"""\\multicolumn{{9}}{{l}}{{\\footnotesize $n = {n_per_cond}$ independent physical evaluations per condition ({total_evals} total evaluations, {warmups_count} warmups excluded).}} \\\\
\\multicolumn{{9}}{{l}}{{\\footnotesize Telemetry Backend: {backend_str}. Data Source: \\texttt{{{datasource_str}}}.}} \\\\
\\hline
\\end{{tabular}}
\\end{{table*}}
"""

    tex_out = os.path.join(output_dir, "tables", "table_journal_ablation.tex")
    with open(tex_out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(latex_table)

    # Generate macros_empirical.tex with all prose-cited empirical numbers
    generate_empirical_macros(summary_stats, comparisons, eval_records, output_dir)

    # Compute Spearman rank correlation matrix across regimes
    compute_spearman_correlation_matrix(
        regime_a_path=input_path,
        regime_b_path=os.path.join(output_dir, "warm_cache_trials.jsonl"),
        output_dir=output_dir,
    )

    print(f"✅ Generated summary JSON: {stats_out}")
    print(f"✅ Generated statistical tests JSON: {tests_out}")
    print(f"✅ Generated CSV table: {csv_out}")
    print(f"✅ Generated LaTeX table: {tex_out}")


def generate_empirical_macros(
    summary_stats: dict[str, Any],
    comparisons: dict[str, Any],
    eval_records: list[dict[str, Any]],
    output_dir: str,
) -> str:
    # Mandatory agent and workload validation
    for ak in ["picoclaw", "openclaw_minimal", "openclaw_full", "neural_baseline"]:
        if ak not in summary_stats:
            raise KeyError(f"Missing mandatory agent in summary_statistics: {ak}")
        for w in ["W1", "W2", "W3", "W4"]:
            if w not in summary_stats[ak]:
                raise KeyError(f"Missing mandatory workload '{w}' for agent '{ak}' in summary_statistics")

    pico_w2 = summary_stats["picoclaw"]["W2"]
    pico_w3 = summary_stats["picoclaw"]["W3"]
    pico_w4 = summary_stats["picoclaw"]["W4"]

    open_min_w1 = summary_stats["openclaw_minimal"]["W1"]
    open_min_w2 = summary_stats["openclaw_minimal"]["W2"]
    open_min_w3 = summary_stats["openclaw_minimal"]["W3"]

    open_full_w1 = summary_stats["openclaw_full"]["W1"]
    open_full_w3 = summary_stats["openclaw_full"]["W3"]
    open_full_w4 = summary_stats["openclaw_full"]["W4"]

    neural_w2 = summary_stats["neural_baseline"]["W2"]

    cmp_w3_min = comparisons.get("PicoClaw_vs_OpenClawMinimal", {}).get("W3")
    cmp_w3_full = comparisons.get("PicoClaw_vs_OpenClawFull", {}).get("W3")
    cmp_w4 = comparisons.get("PicoClaw_vs_OpenClawFull", {}).get("W4")

    if cmp_w3_min is None or cmp_w3_full is None or cmp_w4 is None:
        raise KeyError("Missing mandatory statistical comparison in comparisons")

    pico_trials = [r for r in eval_records if r.get("agent_key") == "picoclaw"]
    open_min_trials = [r for r in eval_records if r.get("agent_key") == "openclaw_minimal"]

    if not pico_trials or not open_min_trials:
        raise ValueError("Missing trial records for picoclaw or openclaw_minimal in eval_records")

    pico_w2_trials = [r for r in pico_trials if r.get("workload") == "W2"]
    open_min_w2_trials = [r for r in open_min_trials if r.get("workload") == "W2"]

    if not pico_w2_trials or not open_min_w2_trials:
        raise ValueError("Missing W2 trial records for picoclaw or openclaw_minimal")

    pico_w2_tokens = float(np.mean([r["completion_tokens"] for r in pico_w2_trials]))
    open_min_w2_tokens = float(np.mean([r["completion_tokens"] for r in open_min_w2_trials]))
    pico_w2_neural_mean = float(np.mean([r["t_neural_ms"] for r in pico_w2_trials]))
    open_min_w2_neural_mean = float(np.mean([r["t_neural_ms"] for r in open_min_w2_trials]))

    pico_inst_m = float(np.mean([r.get("proc_instructions", 0) for r in pico_trials])) / 1e6
    pico_cyc_m = float(np.mean([r.get("proc_cycles", 0) for r in pico_trials])) / 1e6
    open_min_inst_m = float(np.mean([r.get("proc_instructions", 0) for r in open_min_trials])) / 1e6
    open_min_cyc_m = float(np.mean([r.get("proc_cycles", 0) for r in open_min_trials])) / 1e6

    inst_ratio = open_min_inst_m / pico_inst_m if pico_inst_m > 0 else 0.0
    cyc_ratio = open_min_cyc_m / pico_cyc_m if pico_cyc_m > 0 else 0.0

    pico_rss_all = [summary_stats["picoclaw"][w]["rss_peak_mb"] for w in ["W1", "W2", "W3", "W4"]]
    open_rss_all = [summary_stats["openclaw_minimal"][w]["rss_peak_mb"] for w in ["W1", "W2", "W3", "W4"]]

    pico_phys_all = [summary_stats["picoclaw"][w].get("proc_peak_phys_mb", summary_stats["picoclaw"][w]["rss_peak_mb"]) for w in ["W1", "W2", "W3", "W4"]]
    open_phys_all = [summary_stats["openclaw_minimal"][w].get("proc_peak_phys_mb", summary_stats["openclaw_minimal"][w]["rss_peak_mb"]) for w in ["W1", "W2", "W3", "W4"]]

    ram_ratios = [open_rss_all[i] / pico_rss_all[i] for i in range(4)]
    phys_ratios = [open_phys_all[i] / pico_phys_all[i] for i in range(4)]

    pico_phys_m = float(np.mean([r.get("proc_peak_phys_mb", 0) for r in pico_trials]))
    open_min_phys_m = float(np.mean([r.get("proc_peak_phys_mb", 0) for r in open_min_trials]))
    phys_ratio = open_min_phys_m / pico_phys_m if pico_phys_m > 0 else 0.0

    w2_orch_speedup = open_min_w2["t_orch_median_ms"] / pico_w2["t_orch_median_ms"] if pico_w2["t_orch_median_ms"] > 0 else 1.0
    w2_ram_reduction = open_min_w2["rss_peak_mb"] / pico_w2["rss_peak_mb"] if pico_w2["rss_peak_mb"] > 0 else 1.0

    def _fmt_energy(val: float | None) -> str:
        if val is None:
            return r"\textbf{N/A}"
        return f"{val:.2f}"

    def _fmt_ratio(num: float | None, den: float | None) -> str:
        if num is None or den is None or den == 0.0:
            return r"\textbf{N/A}"
        return f"{(num / den):.2f}"

    def _fmt_pct(num: float | None, den: float | None) -> str:
        if num is None or den is None or den == 0.0:
            return r"\textbf{N/A}"
        return f"{((den - num) / den * 100.0):.2f}"

    def _format_p_val(p_val: float) -> str:
        if p_val < 1e-5:
            return r"< 10^{-5}"
        else:
            return f"= {p_val:.4f}"

    p_w3_full_str = _format_p_val(cmp_w3_full["holm_bonferroni_p_value"])
    p_w3_min_str = _format_p_val(cmp_w3_min["holm_bonferroni_p_value"])
    p_w4_str = _format_p_val(cmp_w4["holm_bonferroni_p_value"])

    macros_path = os.path.join(output_dir, "tables", "macros_empirical.tex")
    lines = [
        "% Automatically generated macros from publication campaign empirical results",
        "% DO NOT EDIT MANUALLY - Generated by benchmark/analyze_results.py",
        f"\\newcommand{{\\WThreePicoMedian}}{{{pico_w3['t_task_median_ms']:.1f}}}",
        f"\\newcommand{{\\WThreePicoIQR}}{{{pico_w3['t_task_iqr_ms']:.1f}}}",
        f"\\newcommand{{\\WThreePicoMean}}{{{pico_w3['t_task_mean_ms']:.1f}}}",
        f"\\newcommand{{\\WThreePicoSD}}{{{pico_w3['t_task_std_ms']:.1f}}}",
        f"\\newcommand{{\\WThreeOpenMinMedian}}{{{open_min_w3['t_task_median_ms']:.1f}}}",
        f"\\newcommand{{\\WThreeOpenMinIQR}}{{{open_min_w3['t_task_iqr_ms']:.1f}}}",
        f"\\newcommand{{\\WThreeOpenFullMedian}}{{{open_full_w3['t_task_median_ms']:.1f}}}",
        f"\\newcommand{{\\WThreeOpenFullIQR}}{{{open_full_w3['t_task_iqr_ms']:.1f}}}",
        f"\\newcommand{{\\WThreeCliffsDeltaMin}}{{{cmp_w3_min['cliffs_delta']:.3f}}}",
        f"\\newcommand{{\\WThreeAOneTwoMin}}{{{cmp_w3_min['vargha_delaney_a12']:.3f}}}",
        f"\\newcommand{{\\WThreeAdjPMin}}{{{p_w3_min_str}}}",
        f"\\newcommand{{\\WThreeCliffsDeltaFull}}{{{cmp_w3_full['cliffs_delta']:.3f}}}",
        f"\\newcommand{{\\WThreeAOneTwoFull}}{{{cmp_w3_full['vargha_delaney_a12']:.3f}}}",

def compute_spearman_correlation_matrix(
    regime_a_path: str | None = None,
    regime_b_path: str | None = None,
    output_dir: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Computes pairwise Spearman rank correlation matrix (rho and two-tailed p-values)
    across 12 critical systems telemetry dimensions for Regime A (Cold Prefill)
    and Regime B (Realistic KV-Cache Reuse).
    Outputs results/publication_campaign/spearman_correlation_matrix.json
    and results/publication_campaign/tables/table_spearman_correlation.tex.
    """
    if regime_a_path is None:
        regime_a_path = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "results", "publication_campaign", "raw_trials.jsonl")
    if regime_b_path is None:
        regime_b_path = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "results", "publication_campaign", "warm_cache_trials.jsonl")
    if output_dir is None:
        output_dir = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "results", "publication_campaign")

    def load_trials(p: str) -> list[dict]:
        if not os.path.exists(p):
            return []
        with open(p, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return [
                json.loads(line)
                for line in f
                if line.strip() and not json.loads(line).get("is_warmup", False)
            ]

    recs_a = load_trials(regime_a_path)
    recs_b = load_trials(regime_b_path)

    metric_defs = [
        ("t_task_ms", "Task Latency", "$T_{\\text{task}}$"),
        ("t_neural_ms", "Neural Latency", "$T_{\\text{neural}}$"),
        ("t_orch_ms", "Orch Overhead", "$T_{\\text{orch}}$"),
        ("t_dispatch_ms", "Dispatch Latency", "$T_{\\text{disp}}$"),
        ("t_http_ms", "HTTP Latency", "$T_{\\text{http}}$"),
        ("rss_peak_mb", "Peak RSS", "RSS"),
        ("proc_peak_phys_mb", "Peak Phys RAM", "PhysRAM"),
        ("proc_instructions", "Retired Instructions", "Instr"),
        ("proc_cycles", "CPU Cycles", "Cycles"),
        ("prompt_tokens", "Prompt Tokens", "$N_{\\text{prompt}}$"),
        ("energy_net_above_idle_j", "Net Energy", "$E_{\\text{net}}$"),
        ("edp_js", "Energy-Delay Product", "EDP"),
    ]

    def build_matrix(records: list[dict]):
        matrix = {}
        for m1, name1, sym1 in metric_defs:
            matrix[m1] = {}
            vals1 = [float(r[m1]) for r in records if r.get(m1) is not None]
            for m2, name2, sym2 in metric_defs:
                vals2 = [float(r[m2]) for r in records if r.get(m2) is not None]
                if len(vals1) == len(vals2) and len(vals1) > 2:
                    rho, p_val = stats.spearmanr(vals1, vals2)
                    matrix[m1][m2] = {
                        "rho": round(float(rho), 4),
                        "p_value": round(float(p_val), 6),
                        "significant_001": bool(p_val < 0.001),
                    }
                else:
                    matrix[m1][m2] = {"rho": None, "p_value": None, "significant_001": False}
        return matrix

    mat_a = build_matrix(recs_a) if recs_a else {}
    mat_b = build_matrix(recs_b) if recs_b else {}

    out_json_path = os.path.join(output_dir, "spearman_correlation_matrix.json")
    full_output = {
        "description": "Pairwise Spearman Rank Correlation Matrix across 12 Systems Dimensions",
        "n_evaluations_regime_a": len(recs_a),
        "n_evaluations_regime_b": len(recs_b),
        "metrics": [m[0] for m in metric_defs],
        "metric_labels": {m[0]: m[1] for m in metric_defs},
        "regime_a_cold_prefill": mat_a,
        "regime_b_warm_kv_cache": mat_b,
    }
    with open(out_json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(full_output, f, indent=2)
    print(f"✅ Generated Spearman correlation matrix JSON: {out_json_path}")

    # Generate LaTeX Table
    tex_path = os.path.join(output_dir, "tables", "table_spearman_correlation.tex")
    latex = r"""\begin{table*}[t]
\centering
\caption{Spearman Rank Correlation Matrix ($\rho$) across Key Systems Telemetry Dimensions on Apple Silicon M4. Contrasts Regime A (Cold Prefill, $n=600$) and Regime B (Warm KV-Cache Reuse, $n=600$). In Regime A, prompt attention computation dominates global latency ($T_{\text{task}}$) and energy ($E_{\text{net}}$), yielding near-perfect rank alignment with token length ($\rho > 0.95$). In Regime B, KV-cache amortization unmasks runtime execution dynamics, where retired CPU instructions and dispatch latency strongly decouple from prompt prefill. Statistical significance: $^{***}p < 0.001$, $^{**}p < 0.01$, $^{*}p < 0.05$.}
\label{tab:spearman_correlation}
\small
\begin{tabular}{l r r r r}
\hline
\textbf{Systems Interaction} & \textbf{Regime A ($\rho_{\text{cold}}$)} & \textbf{$p$-value (A)} & \textbf{Regime B ($\rho_{\text{warm}}$)} & \textbf{$p$-value (B)} \\
\hline
"""
    key_pairs = [
        ("t_task_ms", "t_neural_ms", "$T_{\\text{task}}$ vs $T_{\\text{neural}}$ (Inference Latency)"),
        ("t_task_ms", "prompt_tokens", "$T_{\\text{task}}$ vs $N_{\\text{prompt}}$ (Prompt Token Length)"),
        ("t_task_ms", "energy_net_above_idle_j", "$T_{\\text{task}}$ vs $E_{\\text{net}}$ (Net Package Energy)"),
        ("t_task_ms", "edp_js", "$T_{\\text{task}}$ vs EDP (Energy-Delay Product)"),
        ("t_task_ms", "t_orch_ms", "$T_{\\text{task}}$ vs $T_{\\text{orch}}$ (Orchestrator Overhead)"),
        ("t_neural_ms", "prompt_tokens", "$T_{\\text{neural}}$ vs $N_{\\text{prompt}}$ (Attention Prefill)"),
        ("energy_net_above_idle_j", "prompt_tokens", "$E_{\\text{net}}$ vs $N_{\\text{prompt}}$ (Prefill Energy Drain)"),
        ("proc_instructions", "proc_cycles", "Retired Instructions vs CPU Cycles"),
        ("proc_instructions", "t_orch_ms", "Retired Instructions vs $T_{\\text{orch}}$"),
        ("proc_instructions", "rss_peak_mb", "Retired Instructions vs Peak RSS"),
        ("rss_peak_mb", "proc_peak_phys_mb", "Peak RSS vs Peak Physical RAM"),
        ("t_dispatch_ms", "t_orch_ms", "$T_{\\text{disp}}$ vs $T_{\\text{orch}}$ (Serialization Share)"),
    ]

    def fmt_rho(mat, m1, m2):
        if not mat or m1 not in mat or m2 not in mat[m1]:
            return "N/A", "N/A"
        cell = mat[m1][m2]
        r = cell.get("rho")
        p = cell.get("p_value")
        if r is None or p is None:
            return "N/A", "N/A"
        stars = "***" if p < 0.001 else ("**" if p < 0.01 else ("*" if p < 0.05 else ""))
        p_str = "$< 10^{-5}$" if p < 0.00001 else f"{p:.4f}"
        return f"{r:+.3f}$^{{{stars}}}$", p_str

    for m1, m2, label in key_pairs:
        rho_a, p_a = fmt_rho(mat_a, m1, m2)
        rho_b, p_b = fmt_rho(mat_b, m1, m2)
        latex += f"{label} & {rho_a} & {p_a} & {rho_b} & {p_b} \\\\\n"

    latex += r"""\hline
\multicolumn{5}{l}{\footnotesize Non-parametric Spearman rank correlation evaluated over $N=600$ independent physical evaluations per regime ($1{,}200$ total).} \\
\hline
\end{tabular}
\end{table*}
"""
    with open(tex_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(latex)
    print(f"✅ Generated Spearman correlation LaTeX table: {tex_path}")
    return full_output


if __name__ == "__main__":
    in_p = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else None
    out_d = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else None
    analyze_publication_campaign(in_p, out_d)



================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
scripts/run_prefill_ablation.py - Controlled Tool Catalog Schema Prefill & KV Cache Ablation
Ablação Controlada de Prefill de Esquemas de Ferramentas e Reuso de KV-Cache

Evaluates latency scaling of tool schema injection across 8 catalog sizes [0, 5, 10, 15, 25, 40, 54, 100]
and prompt cache configurations [cache_prompt=False, True] directly against llama.cpp Metal GPU server.
Fits Linear, Quadratic, and Power-Law (T = a * N^alpha) models on level medians with 95% bootstrap CIs.
Saves trials to results/publication_campaign/prefill_ablation.jsonl and outputs LaTeX table.

Author: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
"""

import datetime
import json
import os
import sys
import time
import urllib.request

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import curve_fit

REPO_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
API_URL = os.environ.get("LLAMA_API_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:8080/v1/chat/completions")
HEALTH_URL = os.environ.get("LLAMA_HEALTH_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:8080/health")
OUTPUT_DIR = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "results", "publication_campaign")
JSONL_OUT = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, "prefill_ablation.jsonl")
TEX_OUT = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, "tables", "table_prefill_ablation.tex")
FIT_OUT = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, "prefill_regression_fit.json")


def check_server_health() -> bool:
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(HEALTH_URL, timeout=3) as resp:
            if resp.status == 200:
                return True
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"❌ Error: llama-server not responding at {HEALTH_URL}: {e}")
        return False
    return False


def build_tool_catalog(n_tools: int) -> list[dict]:
    if n_tools == 0:
        return []
    catalog = []
    for i in range(n_tools):
        catalog.append({
            "type": "function",
            "function": {
                "name": f"system_tool_{i}",
                "description": f"Standardized benchmark tool descriptor #{i} for system diagnostics and telemetry routing.",
                "parameters": {
                    "type": "object",
                    "properties": {
                        "action": {"type": "string", "description": "Action opcode to execute"},
                        "target": {"type": "string", "description": "Target resource descriptor or file path"}
                    },
                    "required": ["action", "target"]
                }
            }
        })
    return catalog


def call_llama_streaming(messages: list[dict], tools: list[dict], cache_prompt: bool, max_tokens: int = 4) -> dict:
    payload = {
        "model": "Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct-Q4_K_M.gguf",
        "messages": messages,
        "max_tokens": max_tokens,
        "temperature": 0.0,
        "seed": 42,
        "stream": True,
        "cache_prompt": cache_prompt,
    }
    if tools:
        payload["tools"] = tools

    data = json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
    req = urllib.request.Request(API_URL, data=data, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})

    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    first_token_time = None
    prompt_tokens = 0
    cached_tokens = 0
    uncached_prompt_tokens = 0
    completion_tokens = 0
    prompt_ms = 0.0
    predicted_ms = 0.0
    has_timings = False

    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=180) as resp:
        for line in resp:
            line_str = line.decode("utf-8").strip()
            if not line_str.startswith("data: "):
                continue
            body = line_str[6:].strip()
            if body == "[DONE]":
                break
            try:
                chunk = json.loads(body)
                choices = chunk.get("choices", [])
                if choices:
                    delta = choices[0].get("delta", {})
                    content = delta.get("content") or delta.get("tool_calls")
                    if content and first_token_time is None:
                        first_token_time = time.perf_counter()

                timings = chunk.get("timings")
                if timings:
                    has_timings = True
                    prompt_ms = float(timings.get("prompt_ms", prompt_ms))
                    predicted_ms = float(timings.get("predicted_ms", predicted_ms))
                    uncached_prompt_tokens = int(timings.get("prompt_n", uncached_prompt_tokens))
                    cached_tokens = int(timings.get("cache_n", cached_tokens))
                    completion_tokens = int(timings.get("predicted_n", completion_tokens))

                usage = chunk.get("usage")
                if usage:
                    prompt_tokens = int(usage.get("prompt_tokens", prompt_tokens))
                    completion_tokens = int(usage.get("completion_tokens", completion_tokens))
            except Exception:
                pass

    t_end = time.perf_counter()
    t_total_ms = (t_end - t0) * 1000.0
    ttft_ms = ((first_token_time - t0) * 1000.0) if first_token_time else t_total_ms

    if prompt_tokens == 0:
        prompt_tokens = cached_tokens + uncached_prompt_tokens

    if not has_timings:
        prompt_ms = ttft_ms
        predicted_ms = max(0.0, t_total_ms - ttft_ms)

    return {
        "ttft_ms": round(ttft_ms, 2),
        "prompt_ms": round(prompt_ms, 2),
        "predicted_ms": round(predicted_ms, 2),
        "t_total_ms": round(t_total_ms, 2),
        "prompt_tokens": prompt_tokens,
        "cached_tokens": cached_tokens,
        "uncached_prompt_tokens": uncached_prompt_tokens,
        "completion_tokens": completion_tokens,
    }


def power_law_func(x, a, alpha):
    return a * np.power(x, alpha)


def compute_aicc(n: int, k: int, rss: float) -> float:
    if rss <= 0 or n <= k + 1:
        return float("nan")
    aic = n * np.log(rss / n) + 2 * k
    aicc = aic + (2 * k * (k + 1)) / (n - k - 1)
    return float(aicc)


def compute_prefill_regression_fit(records: list[dict]) -> dict:
    # Filter non-warmup cold prefill trials
    cold_trials = [r for r in records if not r.get("is_warmup", False) and not r.get("cache_prompt", False)]
    if not cold_trials:
        cold_trials = [r for r in records if not r.get("cache_prompt", False)]

    # Group by tool_count to compute level medians
    tool_counts = sorted(list({r["tool_count"] for r in cold_trials}))
    medians_n = []
    medians_y = []
    level_data = {}

    for tc in tool_counts:
        subset = [r for r in cold_trials if r["tool_count"] == tc]
        n_p = float(np.median([r["prompt_tokens"] for r in subset]))
        p_ms = float(np.median([r["prompt_ms"] for r in subset]))
        medians_n.append(n_p)
        medians_y.append(p_ms)
        level_data[tc] = subset

    N = np.array(medians_n, dtype=float)
    y = np.array(medians_y, dtype=float)
    n_pts = len(N)
    ss_tot = float(np.sum((y - np.mean(y)) ** 2))

    # 1. Linear Fit: y = beta_1 * N + beta_0 (k=2)
    lin_coeffs = np.polyfit(N, y, 1)
    beta_1 = float(lin_coeffs[0])
    beta_0 = float(lin_coeffs[1])
    y_pred_lin = beta_1 * N + beta_0
    ss_res_lin = float(np.sum((y - y_pred_lin) ** 2))
    r2_lin = 1.0 - (ss_res_lin / ss_tot) if ss_tot > 0 else 1.0
    aicc_lin = compute_aicc(n_pts, 2, ss_res_lin)

    # 2. Quadratic Fit: y = beta_2 * N^2 + beta_1 * N + beta_0 (k=3)
    quad_coeffs = np.polyfit(N, y, 2)
    beta_2_q = float(quad_coeffs[0])
    beta_1_q = float(quad_coeffs[1])
    beta_0_q = float(quad_coeffs[2])
    y_pred_quad = beta_2_q * (N ** 2) + beta_1_q * N + beta_0_q
    ss_res_quad = float(np.sum((y - y_pred_quad) ** 2))
    r2_quad = 1.0 - (ss_res_quad / ss_tot) if ss_tot > 0 else 1.0
    aicc_quad = compute_aicc(n_pts, 3, ss_res_quad)

    # 3. Power-Law Fit: y = a * N^alpha (k=2)
    try:
        popt_pow, _ = curve_fit(power_law_func, N, y, p0=[1.0, 1.2], maxfev=10000)
        a_pow = float(popt_pow[0])
        alpha_pow = float(popt_pow[1])
        y_pred_pow = power_law_func(N, a_pow, alpha_pow)
        ss_res_pow = float(np.sum((y - y_pred_pow) ** 2))
        r2_pow = 1.0 - (ss_res_pow / ss_tot) if ss_tot > 0 else 1.0
        aicc_pow = compute_aicc(n_pts, 2, ss_res_pow)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"⚠️ Power-law fit warning: {e}")
        a_pow, alpha_pow, r2_pow, aicc_pow = float("nan"), float("nan"), float("nan"), float("nan")

    # Bootstrap 95% Confidence Intervals (2000 resamples)
    n_boot = 2000
    rng = np.random.default_rng(42)
    boot_lin_b1, boot_lin_b0 = [], []
    boot_quad_b2, boot_quad_b1, boot_quad_b0 = [], [], []
    boot_pow_a, boot_pow_alpha = [], []

    for _ in range(n_boot):
        resampled_n = []
        resampled_y = []
        for tc in tool_counts:
            trials = level_data[tc]
            sampled_idx = rng.integers(0, len(trials), size=len(trials))
            sampled_tokens = [trials[i]["prompt_tokens"] for i in sampled_idx]
            sampled_ms = [trials[i]["prompt_ms"] for i in sampled_idx]
            resampled_n.append(float(np.median(sampled_tokens)))
            resampled_y.append(float(np.median(sampled_ms)))

        b_N = np.array(resampled_n, dtype=float)
        b_y = np.array(resampled_y, dtype=float)

        # Linear
        try:
            lc = np.polyfit(b_N, b_y, 1)
            boot_lin_b1.append(float(lc[0]))
            boot_lin_b0.append(float(lc[1]))
        except Exception:
            pass

        # Quadratic
        try:
            qc = np.polyfit(b_N, b_y, 2)
            boot_quad_b2.append(float(qc[0]))
            boot_quad_b1.append(float(qc[1]))
            boot_quad_b0.append(float(qc[2]))
        except Exception:
            pass

        # Power law
        try:
            popt, _ = curve_fit(power_law_func, b_N, b_y, p0=[a_pow, alpha_pow], maxfev=5000)
            boot_pow_a.append(float(popt[0]))
            boot_pow_alpha.append(float(popt[1]))
        except Exception:
            pass

    def ci95(arr):
        if len(arr) == 0:
            return [float("nan"), float("nan")]
        return [round(float(np.percentile(arr, 2.5)), 6), round(float(np.percentile(arr, 97.5)), 6)]

    fit_results = {
        "dataset": "results/publication_campaign/prefill_ablation.jsonl",
        "n_levels": n_pts,
        "n_total_eval_samples": len(cold_trials),
        "target_variable": "prompt_ms (T_prefill in milliseconds)",
        "predictor_variable": "prompt_tokens (N)",
        "bootstrap_resamples": n_boot,
        "linear_fit": {
            "formula": "T_prefill = beta_1 * N + beta_0",
            "beta_1": round(beta_1, 6),
            "beta_0": round(beta_0, 4),
            "beta_1_ci95": ci95(boot_lin_b1),
            "beta_0_ci95": ci95(boot_lin_b0),
            "r_squared": round(r2_lin, 5),
            "aicc": round(aicc_lin, 2),
        },
        "quadratic_fit": {
            "formula": "T_prefill = beta_2 * N^2 + beta_1 * N + beta_0",
            "beta_2": round(beta_2_q, 8),
            "beta_1": round(beta_1_q, 6),
            "beta_0": round(beta_0_q, 4),
            "beta_2_ci95": ci95(boot_quad_b2),
            "beta_1_ci95": ci95(boot_quad_b1),
            "beta_0_ci95": ci95(boot_quad_b0),
            "r_squared": round(r2_quad, 5),
            "aicc": round(aicc_quad, 2),
        },
        "power_law_fit": {
            "formula": "T_prefill = a * N^alpha",
            "a": round(a_pow, 6),
            "alpha": round(alpha_pow, 4),
            "a_ci95": ci95(boot_pow_a),
            "alpha_ci95": ci95(boot_pow_alpha),
            "r_squared": round(r2_pow, 5),
            "aicc": round(aicc_pow, 2),
        },
    }

    with open(FIT_OUT, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(fit_results, f, indent=2)
    print(f"✅ Generated prefill regression fit: {FIT_OUT}")
    return fit_results


def generate_latex_table(records: list[dict]):
    latex = r"""\begin{table*}[t]
\centering
\caption{Controlled Tool Catalog Schema Prefill and KV-Cache Reuse Characterization on \texttt{llama.cpp} Metal GPU Server. Evaluates prompt token length ($N_{\text{prompt}}$), Time-to-First-Token (TTFT), and Prefill duration ($T_{\text{prefill}}$) across 8 catalog sizes ($T \in \{0, 5, 10, 15, 25, 40, 54, 100\}$) under Cold Prefill ($\texttt{cache\_prompt} = \text{false}$) versus Warm KV-Cache Reuse ($\texttt{cache\_prompt} = \text{true}$). Values denote empirical median ($n=30$ evaluations per condition following 3 priming warmups).}
\label{tab:prefill_ablation}
\small
\begin{tabular}{r r r r r r r r}
\hline
\textbf{Tools} & \textbf{Prompt Tokens} & \textbf{Cached Tokens (Warm)} & \textbf{Cold TTFT [ms]} & \textbf{Warm TTFT [ms]} & \textbf{Cold Prefill [ms]} & \textbf{Warm Prefill [ms]} & \textbf{Speedup} \\
\hline
"""

    eval_records = [r for r in records if not r.get("is_warmup", False)]
    tool_counts = sorted(list({r["tool_count"] for r in eval_records}))
    if not tool_counts:
        tool_counts = [0, 5, 10, 15, 25, 40, 54, 100]

    for tc in tool_counts:
        cold_recs = [r for r in eval_records if r["tool_count"] == tc and not r["cache_prompt"]]
        warm_recs = [r for r in eval_records if r["tool_count"] == tc and r["cache_prompt"]]

        n_p = int(round(np.median([r["prompt_tokens"] for r in cold_recs]))) if cold_recs else 0
        cached_warm = int(round(np.median([r.get("cached_tokens", 0) for r in warm_recs]))) if warm_recs else 0
        cold_ttft = float(np.median([r["ttft_ms"] for r in cold_recs])) if cold_recs else 0.0
        warm_ttft = float(np.median([r["ttft_ms"] for r in warm_recs])) if warm_recs else 0.0
        cold_prefill = float(np.median([r["prompt_ms"] for r in cold_recs])) if cold_recs else 0.0
        warm_prefill = float(np.median([r["prompt_ms"] for r in warm_recs])) if warm_recs else 0.0

        speedup = (cold_prefill / warm_prefill) if warm_prefill > 0 else (cold_ttft / warm_ttft if warm_ttft > 0 else 1.0)
        latex += f"{tc} & {n_p} & {cached_warm} & {cold_ttft:.1f} & {warm_ttft:.1f} & {cold_prefill:.1f} & {warm_prefill:.1f} & {speedup:.1f}$\\times$ \\\\\n"

    latex += r"""\hline
\multicolumn{8}{l}{\footnotesize Decoded via Apple Silicon M4 Metal GPU; llama.cpp b11887; -c 32768 -np 1 -ngl 99 -t 4; temperature = 0.0, seed = 42.} \\
\hline
\end{tabular}
\end{table*}
"""

    with open(TEX_OUT, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(latex)
    print(f"✅ Generated consolidated LaTeX ablation table: {TEX_OUT}")


def run_prefill_ablation():
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, "tables"), exist_ok=True)

    if "--analyze-only" in sys.argv:
        print(f"📊 Running analysis and table generation on existing {JSONL_OUT}...")
        with open(JSONL_OUT, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            all_records = [json.loads(line) for line in f if line.strip()]
        generate_latex_table(all_records)
        compute_prefill_regression_fit(all_records)
        return

    if not check_server_health():
        sys.exit(1)

    tool_counts = [0, 5, 10, 15, 25, 40, 54, 100]
    cache_modes = [False, True]
    warmup_reps = 3
    eval_reps = 30

    messages = [
        {"role": "system", "content": "You are a concise technical systems assistant."},
        {"role": "user", "content": "State the primary determinant of memory bus contention in multi-core SoCs in one sentence."}
    ]

    print(f"🚀 Running Controlled Prefill & KV-Cache Ablation Campaign ({len(tool_counts)} catalog levels x 2 cache modes x (3 warmups + 30 evals) = {len(tool_counts)*2*(warmup_reps+eval_reps)} requests)...")

    # Wipe or initialize output JSONL for fresh, clean physical run
    all_records = []
    with open(JSONL_OUT, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        pass

    for tc in tool_counts:
        tools = build_tool_catalog(tc)
        print(f"\n📦 Catalog Level: {tc} tools")

        for cache_prompt in cache_modes:
            mode_label = "Warm (KV Cache Reuse)" if cache_prompt else "Cold (Prompt Prefill)"
            print(f"  ⚡ Cache Mode: {mode_label}")

            # 1. Warmups (isolated priming block)
            for w in range(1, warmup_reps + 1):
                metrics = call_llama_streaming(messages, tools, cache_prompt=cache_prompt, max_tokens=4)
                rec = {
                    "timestamp_iso": datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                    "tool_count": tc,
                    "cache_prompt": cache_prompt,
                    "rep": w,
                    "is_warmup": True,
                    **metrics,
                }
                all_records.append(rec)
                with open(JSONL_OUT, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    f.write(json.dumps(rec) + "\n")

            # 2. Evaluations (consecutive block)
            for rep in range(1, eval_reps + 1):
                metrics = call_llama_streaming(messages, tools, cache_prompt=cache_prompt, max_tokens=4)
                rec = {
                    "timestamp_iso": datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                    "tool_count": tc,
                    "cache_prompt": cache_prompt,
                    "rep": rep,
                    "is_warmup": False,
                    **metrics,
                }
                all_records.append(rec)
                with open(JSONL_OUT, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    f.write(json.dumps(rec) + "\n")

            # Summary for this condition block
            eval_subset = [r for r in all_records if r["tool_count"] == tc and r["cache_prompt"] == cache_prompt and not r["is_warmup"]]
            ttft_med = np.median([r["ttft_ms"] for r in eval_subset])
            prefill_med = np.median([r["prompt_ms"] for r in eval_subset])
            tok_med = int(np.median([r["prompt_tokens"] for r in eval_subset]))
            cached_med = int(np.median([r["cached_tokens"] for r in eval_subset]))
            print(f"    -> Median Tokens: {tok_med} (Cached: {cached_med}) | TTFT: {ttft_med:.1f} ms | Prefill: {prefill_med:.1f} ms")

    print(f"\n✅ Prefill ablation completed successfully! Saved {len(all_records)} trials to {JSONL_OUT}")

    # Generate outputs
    generate_latex_table(all_records)
    compute_prefill_regression_fit(all_records)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_prefill_ablation()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
scripts/run_warm_cache_campaign.py - Full Warm-Cache Benchmark Campaign (n=30)
Executes publication campaign with BENCH_CACHE_PROMPT=true on Apple Silicon M4,
capturing SMC hardware energy, process memory, and token throughput.
Outputs:
  - results/publication_campaign/warm_cache_trials.jsonl
  - results/publication_campaign/warm_cache_summary.json
  - results/publication_campaign/warm_cache_statistical_tests.json
  - results/publication_campaign/tables/table_warm_cache.tex
  - results/publication_campaign/campaign_metadata.json
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import random
import sys
import time
import urllib.request
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
from scipy import stats

REPO_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
if REPO_DIR not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, REPO_DIR)

from benchmark.adapters import get_adapter  # noqa: E402
from benchmark.analyze_results import (  # noqa: E402
    bootstrap_ci,
    cliffs_delta,
    holm_bonferroni_adjust,
    vargha_delaney_a12,
)
from benchmark.run_unified_benchmark import collect_host_metadata  # noqa: E402
from benchmark.telemetry import SMCPowerMonitor  # noqa: E402


def check_server_health(url: str = "http://127.0.0.1:8080/health") -> bool:
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(url)
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=3.0) as resp:
            return resp.status == 200
    except Exception:
        return False


def generate_warm_cache_latex_table(
    summary_stats: dict[str, dict[str, dict[str, Any]]],
    output_path: str,
    n_per_cond: int = 30,
    total_evals: int = 600,
    warmups_count: int = 60,
    telemetry_backend: str = "Apple Silicon SMC (50 ms)",
    data_source: str = "measured_hardware_warm_cache",
) -> str:
    workloads = ["W1", "W2", "W3", "W4"]
    configs = [
        ("neural_baseline", "Neural Baseline (Direct)"),
        ("picoclaw", "PicoClaw (Go Native)"),
        ("openclaw_minimal", "OpenClaw (Node.js Minimal)"),
        ("openclaw_full", "OpenClaw (Node.js Full 54-Tool)"),
        ("python_native", "PythonNative (CPython Reference)"),
    ]

    latex_table = r"""\begin{table*}[t]
\centering
\caption{Controlled Performance Evaluation under Realistic KV-Cache Reuse Regime (\texttt{cache\_prompt=true}) on Apple Silicon M4. When prompt prefill is amortized via deterministic block-isolated KV-cache retention, orchestrator overhead ($\Phi_{\text{orch}} = T_{\text{orch}} / T_{\text{task}}$) becomes discriminative between compiled native and interpreted runtimes. Net Energy is integrated above quiescent idle baseline via Apple SMC hardware shunts at 50~ms resolution. Peak Phys RAM denotes lifetime maximum resident physical footprint via Darwin kernel \texttt{libproc}.}
\label{tab:evaluation_warm_cache_m4}
\small
\begin{tabular}{l l c r r r r r r r}
\hline
\textbf{Workload} & \textbf{Configuration} & \textbf{Prompt (Tot/Cache)} & \textbf{Cache Hit [\%]} & \textbf{Median (IQR) [ms]} & \textbf{Disp / HTTP [ms]} & \textbf{Orch Share $\Phi_{\text{orch}}$ [\%]} & \textbf{Peak Phys [MB]} & \textbf{Net Energy [J]} & \textbf{EDP [J$\cdot$s]} \\
\hline
"""

    for w_idx, w in enumerate(workloads):
        for ak, label in configs:
            s = summary_stats.get(ak, {}).get(w, {})
            pt = int(round(s.get("prompt_tokens_mean", 0)))
            cached_t = int(round(s.get("cached_tokens_mean", 0)))
            tokens_str = f"{pt} / {cached_t}"

            cache_hit = s.get("cache_hit_pct_mean", 0.0)
            cache_hit_str = f"{cache_hit:.1f}\\%"

            task_med = s.get("t_task_median_ms", 0.0)
            task_iqr = s.get("t_task_iqr_ms", 0.0)
            lat_str = f"{task_med:.1f} ({task_iqr:.1f})"

            disp_m = s.get("t_dispatch_mean_ms", 0.0)
            http_m = s.get("t_http_mean_ms", 0.0)
            disp_http_str = f"{disp_m:.2f} / {http_m:.2f}"

            phi_orch = s.get("phi_orch_pct_mean", 0.0)
            phi_str = f"{phi_orch:.2f}\\%"

            phys_mb = s.get("proc_peak_phys_mb_mean", s.get("rss_peak_mb", 0.0))
            phys_str = f"{phys_mb:.1f}"

            e_net = s.get("energy_net_mean_j")
            e_str = f"{e_net:.2f}" if e_net is not None else r"\textbf{N/A}"

            edp = s.get("edp_net_mean_js")
            edp_str = f"{edp:.2f}" if edp is not None else r"\textbf{N/A}"

            latex_table += f"{w} & {label} & {tokens_str} & {cache_hit_str} & {lat_str} & {disp_http_str} & {phi_str} & {phys_str} & {e_str} & {edp_str} \\\\\n"

        if w_idx < len(workloads) - 1:
            latex_table += "\\hline\n"

    latex_table += "\\hline\n"
    latex_table += (
        f"\\multicolumn{{10}}{{l}}{{\\footnotesize $n = {n_per_cond}$ independent physical evaluations per condition "
        f"({total_evals} total evaluations, {warmups_count} priming warmups excluded).}} \\\\\n"
    )
    backend_desc = (
        r"Apple Silicon SMC (\texttt{powermetrics} 50~ms current shunt sampling) + Darwin XNU \texttt{libproc}"
        if "smc" in telemetry_backend.lower()
        else telemetry_backend
    )
    safe_data_source = data_source.replace("_", r"\_")
    latex_table += (
        f"\\multicolumn{{10}}{{l}}{{\\footnotesize Telemetry Backend: {backend_desc}. "
        f"Data Source: \\texttt{{{safe_data_source}}}.}} \\\\\n"
    )
    latex_table += "\\hline\n"
    latex_table += "\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table*}\n"

    with open(output_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(latex_table)

    return latex_table


def main():
    print("========================================================================")
    print(" 🚀 Full Warm-Cache Benchmark Campaign (Apple Silicon M4)")
    print("    Regime B: Realistic KV-Cache Reuse (BENCH_CACHE_PROMPT=true)")
    print("========================================================================")

    output_dir = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "results", "publication_campaign")
    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(output_dir, "tables"), exist_ok=True)
    warm_cache_trials_path = os.path.join(output_dir, "warm_cache_trials.jsonl")

    agents = ["picoclaw", "openclaw_minimal", "openclaw_full", "python_native", "neural_baseline"]
    workloads = ["W1", "W2", "W3", "W4"]
    reps_per_condition = 30
    total_evals = len(agents) * len(workloads) * reps_per_condition

    if "--analyze-only" in sys.argv:
        print("📊 [INFO] Running in --analyze-only mode on existing warm_cache_trials.jsonl...")
        if not os.path.exists(warm_cache_trials_path):
            print(f"❌ [FATAL] {warm_cache_trials_path} not found.")
            sys.exit(1)
        all_trials = []
        with open(warm_cache_trials_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for line in f:
                if line.strip():
                    all_trials.append(json.loads(line))
        warmup_records = [t for t in all_trials if t.get("is_warmup")]
        eval_records = [t for t in all_trials if not t.get("is_warmup")]
        telemetry_backend = eval_records[0].get("telemetry_backend", "Apple Silicon SMC (50 ms)") if eval_records else "Apple Silicon SMC (50 ms)"
    else:
        # Enforce BENCH_CACHE_PROMPT=true
        os.environ["BENCH_CACHE_PROMPT"] = "true"

        health_url = "http://127.0.0.1:8080/health"
        if not check_server_health(health_url):
            print(f"❌ [FATAL] llama-server unreachable at {health_url}")
            sys.exit(1)

        smc_monitor = SMCPowerMonitor(sample_interval_ms=50)
        telemetry_backend = "unprivileged_no_smc"
        if smc_monitor.active:
            print("⚡ [INFO] Apple SMC hardware telemetry active (root powermetrics shunts).")
            print("         Measuring 3-second quiescent idle baseline...")
            idle_mw = smc_monitor.measure_idle_baseline(3.0)
            print(f"         Quiescent idle baseline package power: {idle_mw:.2f} mW.")
            telemetry_backend = smc_monitor.telemetry_backend
            print("         Starting continuous Apple SMC powermetrics sampler daemon...")
            smc_monitor.start_campaign()
        else:
            print("⚠️  [NOTICE] Running unprivileged without root SMC shunts.")

        _ = collect_host_metadata()

        adapter_instances = {}
        for ak in agents:
            ad = get_adapter(ak, REPO_DIR)
            if not ad.setup():
                raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to setup adapter '{ak}'.")
            adapter_instances[ak] = ad

        warmup_records = []
        eval_records = []
        rng = random.Random(42)

        conditions = [(ak, w) for ak in agents for w in workloads]
        rng.shuffle(conditions)
        warmups_count = len(conditions) * 3
        trial_idx = 0

        print(f"\n▶️  Dispatching 20 randomized blocks ({reps_per_condition} evaluations + 3 primings per block = {total_evals} evals, {warmups_count} primings)...")

        try:
            for block_idx, (ak, w) in enumerate(conditions, start=1):
                ad = adapter_instances[ak]
                print(f"\n📦 [Block {block_idx:02d}/20] Condition: {ak} on {w} (3 Priming Warmups + {reps_per_condition} Evaluations)")

                # Priming warmups for this block
                for w_rep in range(1, 4):
                    res = ad.run_workload(w, iterations=1)
                    cached_tokens = res.get("cached_tokens", 0)
                    uncached_prompt_tokens = res.get("uncached_prompt_tokens", 0)
                    prompt_tokens = res.get("prompt_tokens", 0)
                    cache_hit_pct = (cached_tokens / prompt_tokens * 100.0) if prompt_tokens > 0 else 0.0

                    rec = {
                        "timestamp_iso": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),
                        "agent_key": ak,
                        "architecture": ad.name,
                        "runtime": ad.runtime,
                        "mode": getattr(ad, "mode", "standard"),
                        "workload": w,
                        "rep": w_rep,
                        "is_warmup": True,
                        "is_simulated": False,
                        "data_source": "measured_hardware_warm_cache_warmup",
                        "t_task_ms": round(res.get("t_task_ms", 0.0), 2),
                        "t_neural_ms": round(res.get("t_neural_ms", 0.0), 2),
                        "t_orch_ms": round(res.get("t_orch_ms", 0.0), 3),
                        "t_dispatch_ms": round(res.get("t_dispatch_ms", 0.0), 3),
                        "t_http_ms": round(res.get("t_http_ms", 0.0), 3),
                        "rss_peak_mb": round(res.get("rss_peak_mb", 0.0), 2),
                        "proc_rss_mb": round(res.get("proc_rss_mb", res.get("rss_peak_mb", 0.0)), 2),
                        "proc_instructions": res.get("proc_instructions", 0),
                        "proc_cycles": res.get("proc_cycles", 0),
                        "proc_billed_energy": res.get("proc_billed_energy", 0),
                        "proc_peak_phys_mb": round(res.get("proc_peak_phys_mb", 0.0), 2),
                        "prompt_tokens": prompt_tokens,
                        "completion_tokens": res.get("completion_tokens", res.get("output_tokens", 0)),
                        "output_tokens": res.get("output_tokens", 0),
                        "cached_tokens": cached_tokens,
                        "uncached_prompt_tokens": uncached_prompt_tokens,
                        "cache_hit_pct": round(cache_hit_pct, 2),
                        "tool_calls": res.get("tool_calls", 0),
                        "http_status": res.get("http_status", 200),
                        "finish_reason": res.get("finish_reason", "stop"),
                        "energy_total_j": None,
                        "energy_net_above_idle_j": None,
                        "edp_js": None,
                    }
                    warmup_records.append(rec)
                    print(f"   [Priming {w_rep}/3] {ak:16s} {w}: Task={rec['t_task_ms']}ms, Prompt={prompt_tokens}, Cached={cached_tokens} ({cache_hit_pct:.1f}%)")

                # 30 consecutive evaluated repetitions for this condition
                for eval_rep in range(1, reps_per_condition + 1):
                    trial_idx += 1
                    t_start_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
                    t0_trial = time.monotonic()
                    res = ad.run_workload(w, iterations=1)
                    t_elapsed_s = time.monotonic() - t0_trial
                    t_end_ns = time.monotonic_ns()

                    energy_dict = None
                    if smc_monitor.active:
                        energy_dict = smc_monitor.slice_energy(t_start_ns, t_end_ns)

                    t_task_ms = res.get("t_task_ms", t_elapsed_s * 1000.0)
                    t_neural_ms = res.get("t_neural_ms", 0.0)
                    t_orch_ms = res.get("t_orch_ms", max(0.0, t_task_ms - t_neural_ms))
                    t_dispatch_ms = res.get("t_dispatch_ms", 0.0)
                    t_http_ms = res.get("t_http_ms", max(0.0, t_orch_ms - t_dispatch_ms))
                    rss_peak = res.get("rss_peak_mb", 0.0)
                    tokens = res.get("output_tokens", 0)
                    prompt_tokens = res.get("prompt_tokens", 0)
                    completion_tokens = res.get("completion_tokens", tokens)
                    cached_tokens = res.get("cached_tokens", 0)
                    uncached_prompt_tokens = res.get("uncached_prompt_tokens", 0)
                    cache_hit_pct = (cached_tokens / prompt_tokens * 100.0) if prompt_tokens > 0 else 0.0

                    energy_total_j = None
                    energy_net_j = None
                    edp_js = None

                    if energy_dict is not None and energy_dict.get("energy_total_j") is not None:
                        energy_total_j = energy_dict["energy_total_j"]
                        energy_net_j = energy_dict["energy_net_above_idle_j"]
                        edp_js = round(energy_net_j * (t_task_ms / 1000.0), 4)

                    phi_orch_pct = (t_orch_ms / t_task_ms * 100.0) if t_task_ms > 0 else 0.0

                    trial_record = {
                        "timestamp_iso": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),
                        "agent_key": ak,
                        "architecture": ad.name,
                        "runtime": ad.runtime,
                        "mode": getattr(ad, "mode", "standard"),
                        "workload": w,
                        "rep": eval_rep,
                        "is_warmup": False,
                        "is_simulated": False,
                        "data_source": "measured_hardware_warm_cache",
                        "telemetry_backend": telemetry_backend,
                        "t_task_ms": round(t_task_ms, 2),
                        "t_neural_ms": round(t_neural_ms, 2),
                        "t_orch_ms": round(t_orch_ms, 3),
                        "phi_orch_pct": round(phi_orch_pct, 3),
                        "ttft_ms": round(res.get("ttft_ms", 0.0), 2),
                        "itl_ms": round(res.get("itl_ms", 0.0), 2),
                        "t_dispatch_ms": round(t_dispatch_ms, 3),
                        "t_http_ms": round(t_http_ms, 3),
                        "r_tool_ops_sec": round(res.get("r_tool_ops_sec", 0.0), 2),
                        "rss_idle_mb": round(res.get("rss_idle_mb", 0.0), 2),
                        "rss_peak_mb": round(rss_peak, 2),
                        "proc_rss_mb": round(res.get("proc_rss_mb", rss_peak), 2),
                        "proc_instructions": res.get("proc_instructions", 0),
                        "proc_cycles": res.get("proc_cycles", 0),
                        "proc_billed_energy": res.get("proc_billed_energy", 0),
                        "proc_peak_phys_mb": round(res.get("proc_peak_phys_mb", 0.0), 2),
                        "prompt_tokens": prompt_tokens,
                        "completion_tokens": completion_tokens,
                        "output_tokens": tokens,
                        "cached_tokens": cached_tokens,
                        "uncached_prompt_tokens": uncached_prompt_tokens,
                        "cache_hit_pct": round(cache_hit_pct, 2),
                        "tool_calls": res.get("tool_calls", 0),
                        "http_status": res.get("http_status", 200),
                        "finish_reason": res.get("finish_reason", "stop"),
                        "energy_total_j": energy_total_j,
                        "energy_net_above_idle_j": energy_net_j,
                        "edp_js": edp_js,
                        "edp_net_js": edp_js,
                    }
                    eval_records.append(trial_record)

                    if eval_rep % 10 == 0 or eval_rep == reps_per_condition:
                        e_disp = f"{energy_net_j:.2f}J" if energy_net_j is not None else "N/A"
                        print(
                            f"   [{trial_idx:03d}/{total_evals:03d}] {ak:16s} {w} Rep {eval_rep:02d}: "
                            f"Task={trial_record['t_task_ms']:6.1f}ms, Orch={trial_record['t_orch_ms']:5.2f}ms, "
                            f"CacheHit={cache_hit_pct:5.1f}%, Φ_orch={phi_orch_pct:4.1f}%, NetE={e_disp}"
                        )

                    time.sleep(0.02)

        finally:
            if smc_monitor.active:
                smc_monitor.stop_campaign()

        # Write all 660 trials to warm_cache_trials.jsonl
        all_trials = warmup_records + eval_records
        print(f"\n💾 Writing {len(all_trials)} total records to {warm_cache_trials_path}...")
        with open(warm_cache_trials_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for t in all_trials:
                f.write(json.dumps(t) + "\n")

    # Phase 3: Analytical Processing
    print("\n📈 Processing warm-cache summary statistics and hypothesis tests...")
    summary_stats = {ak: {} for ak in agents}

    for ak in agents:
        for w in workloads:
            subset = [r for r in eval_records if r["agent_key"] == ak and r["workload"] == w]
            if not subset:
                continue

            tasks = [r["t_task_ms"] for r in subset]
            orchs = [r["t_orch_ms"] for r in subset]
            phis = [r["phi_orch_pct"] for r in subset]
            disps = [r["t_dispatch_ms"] for r in subset]
            https = [r["t_http_ms"] for r in subset]
            phys = [r.get("proc_peak_phys_mb", r["rss_peak_mb"]) for r in subset]
            rsss = [r["rss_peak_mb"] for r in subset]
            pts = [r["prompt_tokens"] for r in subset]
            cts = [r["completion_tokens"] for r in subset]
            cached_toks = [r.get("cached_tokens", 0) for r in subset]
            uncached_toks = [r.get("uncached_prompt_tokens", 0) for r in subset]
            cache_hits = [r.get("cache_hit_pct", 0.0) for r in subset]

            q25, q50, q75 = np.percentile(tasks, [25, 50, 75])
            iqr = q75 - q25

            energies_net = [r["energy_net_above_idle_j"] for r in subset if r.get("energy_net_above_idle_j") is not None]
            edps = [r["edp_js"] for r in subset if r.get("edp_js") is not None]

            summary_stats[ak][w] = {
                "t_task_median_ms": float(q50),
                "t_task_iqr_ms": float(iqr),
                "t_task_mean_ms": float(np.mean(tasks)),
                "t_task_std_ms": float(np.std(tasks, ddof=1)),
                "t_task_p95_ms": float(np.percentile(tasks, 95)),
                "t_orch_median_ms": float(np.median(orchs)),
                "t_orch_iqr_ms": float(np.percentile(orchs, 75) - np.percentile(orchs, 25)),
                "t_orch_mean_ms": float(np.mean(orchs)),
                "phi_orch_pct_mean": float(np.mean(phis)),
                "phi_orch_pct_median": float(np.median(phis)),
                "t_dispatch_mean_ms": float(np.mean(disps)),
                "t_http_mean_ms": float(np.mean(https)),
                "proc_peak_phys_mb_mean": float(np.mean(phys)),
                "proc_peak_phys_mb_max": float(np.max(phys)),
                "rss_peak_mb": float(np.mean(rsss)),
                "prompt_tokens_mean": float(np.mean(pts)),
                "completion_tokens_mean": float(np.mean(cts)),
                "cached_tokens_mean": float(np.mean(cached_toks)),
                "uncached_prompt_tokens_mean": float(np.mean(uncached_toks)),
                "cache_hit_pct_mean": float(np.mean(cache_hits)),
                "energy_net_mean_j": float(np.mean(energies_net)) if energies_net else None,
                "energy_net_std_j": float(np.std(energies_net, ddof=1)) if len(energies_net) > 1 else None,
                "edp_net_mean_js": float(np.mean(edps)) if edps else None,
                "edp_net_median_js": float(np.median(edps)) if edps else None,
            }

    # Save warm_cache_summary.json
    summary_path = os.path.join(output_dir, "warm_cache_summary.json")
    with open(summary_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(summary_stats, f, indent=2)
    print(f"✅ Generated summary JSON: {summary_path}")

    # Hypothesis testing vs. PicoClaw
    raw_p_values = []
    comparison_meta = []
    comparisons = {}

    for w in workloads:
        pico_tasks = [r["t_task_ms"] for r in eval_records if r["agent_key"] == "picoclaw" and r["workload"] == w]
        for ak in ["openclaw_minimal", "openclaw_full", "python_native", "neural_baseline"]:
            other_tasks = [r["t_task_ms"] for r in eval_records if r["agent_key"] == ak and r["workload"] == w]
            u_stat, p_val = stats.mannwhitneyu(pico_tasks, other_tasks, alternative="two-sided")
            d_val = cliffs_delta(other_tasks, pico_tasks)
            a12_val = vargha_delaney_a12(other_tasks, pico_tasks)
            ci_low, ci_high = bootstrap_ci(other_tasks, n_resamples=2000, alpha=0.05, seed=42)

            comp_key = f"picoclaw_vs_{ak}_{w}"
            raw_p_values.append(p_val)
            comparison_meta.append((
                comp_key,
                {
                    "workload": w,
                    "reference": "picoclaw",
                    "comparison": ak,
                    "mann_whitney_u": float(u_stat),
                    "raw_p_value": float(p_val),
                    "cliffs_delta": round(float(d_val), 4),
                    "vargha_delaney_a12": round(float(a12_val), 4),
                    "bootstrap_ci_95": [round(float(ci_low), 4), round(float(ci_high), 4)],
                },
            ))

    adjusted_p = holm_bonferroni_adjust(raw_p_values)
    for (k, data), adj_p in zip(comparison_meta, adjusted_p):
        data["holm_bonferroni_p_value"] = float(adj_p)
        comparisons[k] = data

    tests_path = os.path.join(output_dir, "warm_cache_statistical_tests.json")
    with open(tests_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(comparisons, f, indent=2)
    print(f"✅ Generated statistical tests JSON: {tests_path}")

    # Generate table_warm_cache.tex
    tex_path = os.path.join(output_dir, "tables", "table_warm_cache.tex")
    generate_warm_cache_latex_table(
        summary_stats,
        tex_path,
        n_per_cond=reps_per_condition,
        total_evals=len(eval_records),
        warmups_count=len(warmup_records),
        telemetry_backend=telemetry_backend,
        data_source=eval_records[0].get("data_source", "measured_hardware_warm_cache") if eval_records else "measured_hardware_warm_cache",
    )
    print(f"✅ Generated LaTeX table: {tex_path}")

    # Phase 4: Create campaign_metadata.json
    meta_path = os.path.join(output_dir, "campaign_metadata.json")

    # Calculate total net energy consumed across campaigns
    total_net_energy_j = 0.0
    for p in [os.path.join(output_dir, "raw_trials.jsonl"), warm_cache_trials_path]:
        if os.path.exists(p):
            with open(p, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                for line in f:
                    if line.strip():
                        t_rec = json.loads(line)
                        e_val = t_rec.get("energy_net_above_idle_j")
                        if e_val is not None:
                            total_net_energy_j += float(e_val)

    metadata = {
        "model": "Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct-Q4_K_M.gguf",
        "model_architecture": "Llama-3.2 (3B parameters, GGUF Q4_K_M)",
        "server": {
            "engine": "llama.cpp b11887",
            "cli_arguments": "-c 32768 -np 1 -ngl 99 -t 4",
            "context_window_tokens": 32768,
            "gpu_layers": 99,
            "cpu_threads": 4,
            "parallel_slots": 1,
        },
        "hyperparameters": {
            "temperature": 0.0,
            "seed": 42,
        },
        "regimes": {
            "regime_a_cold_prefill": {
                "description": "Cold prompt prefill baseline without KV-cache reuse",
                "cache_prompt": False,
                "reps_per_condition": 30,
                "total_evaluations": 600,
                "warmups_per_condition": 3,
                "total_warmups": 60,
                "raw_file": "results/publication_campaign/raw_trials.jsonl",
                "table_file": "results/publication_campaign/tables/table_journal_ablation.tex",
            },
            "regime_b_warm_cache": {
                "description": "Realistic KV-cache prefix retention regime with block-isolation cache protocol",
                "cache_prompt": True,
                "reps_per_condition": 30,
                "total_evaluations": 600,
                "warmups_per_condition": 3,
                "total_warmups": 60,
                "caching_protocol": "Block-isolation cache evaluation (-np 1, 3 primings per condition block, 30 consecutive evaluations)",
                "raw_file": "results/publication_campaign/warm_cache_trials.jsonl",
                "table_file": "results/publication_campaign/tables/table_warm_cache.tex",
            },
        },
        "w2_resampling_documentation": {
            "session": "Revision 5 Physical Resampling",
            "reason": "Corrected SSE streaming parser for prompt_tokens under tool_calls responses to ensure full prompt length (>2290 tokens) was captured without dropping to 1 token.",
            "trials_resampled": 150,
            "warmups_resampled": 15,
            "backup_preserved": "results/exploratory_campaigns/raw_trials_pre_w2_fix.jsonl",
        },
        "total_campaign_net_energy_joules": round(total_net_energy_j, 2),
        "hardware": {
            "platform": "Apple Mac mini M4 (Mac16,10)",
            "soc": "Apple M4 (10-Core CPU: 4 Performance + 6 Efficiency)",
            "gpu": "10-Core Apple Metal GPU",
            "memory": "24 GB Unified LPDDR5X UMA @ 120 GB/s bandwidth",
            "os": "macOS 15.x (darwin-arm64)",
            "telemetry": "Apple Silicon SMC (powermetrics 50 ms current shunts) + Darwin XNU libproc (RUSAGE_INFO_V4)",
        },
    }
    with open(meta_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(metadata, f, indent=2)
    print(f"✅ Generated campaign metadata JSON: {meta_path}")

    print("\n========================================================================")
    print(" ✅ Warm-Cache Campaign and Artifacts Consolidated Successfully!")
    print("========================================================================")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: scripts/verify_manifest.py
SHA-256: f4d3724571a6acd241c0954ed7de3dbd2f942ca9aff438f01c64d03154e99f5c
